        UpdateHookCounter = function()
            local playerGui2 = localPlayer2:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if not playerGui2 then
                return
            end
            local v63, v64, v65 = v8(playerGui2:GetChildren())
            local v66 = v65

                    break

                if v70:IsA("ScreenGui") and v70.Name:match("%-mob$") then
                    local frame = v70:FindFirstChild("Frame")

                    if frame then
                        local n3 = 0
                        local v71 = v69
                        local v72 = v67
                        local v73 = v68

                        while true do
                            local n4 = n3 + 1
                            if n4 > 5 then
                                break
                            end
                            n3 = n4

                            local imageLabel = v74 and v74:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel")
                            local textLabel = v74 and v74:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")

                            if imageLabel and textLabel then
                                local mwdCustomHookCounter = imageLabel:FindFirstChild("MWD_CustomHookCounter")
                                local v75 = mwdCustomHookCounter
                                local text = textLabel.Text
                                local v76 = nil

                                for k, v77 in v8(fn()) do
                                    if v77.Name == text or v77.DisplayName == text then
                                        v76 = v77
                                        break
                                    end
                                    continue
                                end

                                n3 = n4
                                local n5 = 0

                                if v76 then
                                    local attribute = v76:GetAttribute("HookCount")
                                    local attribute2 = attribute

                                    if not attribute then
                                        local character = v76.Character
                                        attribute2 = character and v76.Character:GetAttribute("HookCount")
                                    end

                                    if not attribute2 then
                                        attribute2 = 0
                                    end

                                    n5 = attribute2
                                end

                                if not mwdCustomHookCounter then
                                    local TextLabel = new("TextLabel", imageLabel)
                                    v75 = TextLabel
                                    TextLabel.Name = "MWD_CustomHookCounter"
                                    TextLabel.Size = new4(1, 0, 0.35, 0)
                                    TextLabel.Position = new4(0, 0, 0.65, 0)
                                    TextLabel.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                    TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
                                    TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                                    TextLabel.TextScaled = true
                                    TextLabel.Font = font.SourceSansBold
                                end

                                v75.Visible = true

                                if n5 >= 3 then
                                    v75.Text = "DEAD"
                                    v75.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 75, 75)
                                else
                                    v75.Text = "Hooks: " .. v59(n5)

                                    if n5 == 2 then
                                        v75.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 140, 0)
                                    elseif n5 == 1 then
                                        v75.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
                                    else
                                        v75.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        v66 = v71
                        v67 = v72
                        v68 = v73
                    end
                end
            end
        end

            if not humanoidRootPart then

            end
            local veilFov = tbl9.Veil_FOV
            local v64 = nil
            local currentCamera2 = v2.CurrentCamera
            local v65 = new5(currentCamera2.ViewportSize.X / 2, currentCamera2.ViewportSize.Y / 2)

            for k, v66 in v8(fn()) do
                if v66 ~= localPlayer2 and v66.Team and v66.Team.Name == "Survivors" and v66.Character then
                    local character2 = v66.Character
                    local humanoid = character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local v67 = getTargetPartObject(character2)

                    if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and v67 and (v67.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= tbl9.SPEAR_MaxDist then
                        local v68, v69 = currentCamera2:WorldToViewportPoint(v67.Position)

                        if v69 then
                            local magnitude = (new5(v68.X, v68.Y) - v65).Magnitude

                            if magnitude <= veilFov then
                                veilFov = magnitude
                                v64 = v67
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

        end

            end

            if tbl4.FlowstateEnabled then

            end

            end
        end

            fn32(localPlayer2.Character)
        end

            if tbl14.Stalk then

        local tbl18 = {Enabled = false}

        fn10 = function()
            local localPlayer3 = v32.Players.LocalPlayer

            local function fn33()
                repeat

                until localPlayer3.Character and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")

                wait(0.1)

                    MeshPart.CanCollide = false

                    MeshPart.TextureID = "rbxassetid://902843398"

                    WeldConstraint.Part0 = character["Right Leg"]
                    WeldConstraint.Part1 = MeshPart
                    WeldConstraint.Parent = MeshPart
                end)
            end

            fn33()

            if tbl18.Connection then
                tbl18.Connection:Disconnect()

            tbl18.Connection = localPlayer3.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()

            end)

        local ScreenGui = nil
        local tbl19 = {}

        fn11 = function()
            if ScreenGui then
                ScreenGui:Destroy()
                ScreenGui = nil
            end

            tbl19 = {}
            tbl10.created = false
            tbl10.LastCrosshairStyle = nil
        end

        local function fn33()
            if not tbl5.Enabled then
                if ScreenGui then
                    ScreenGui.Enabled = false
                end

                return
            end

            if ScreenGui then
                ScreenGui.Enabled = true
            end

            if tbl10.LastCrosshairStyle ~= tbl5.Style or not ScreenGui then
                fn11()
                tbl10.LastCrosshairStyle = tbl5.Style
            end

            if not tbl10.created then
                tbl10.created = true
                ScreenGui = new("ScreenGui")
                ScreenGui.Name = "CrosshairUI"
                ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
                ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
                ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999
                ScreenGui.Parent = CoreGui

                local function fn34(size, position)
                    local Frame = new("Frame")
                    Frame.BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.Color
                    Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    Frame.AnchorPoint = new5(0.5, 0.5)
                    Frame.Size = size
                    Frame.Position = position
                    Frame.Parent = ScreenGui
                    return Frame
                end

                local v64 = new4(0.5, tbl5.OffsetX, 0.5, tbl5.OffsetY)

                if tbl5.Style == "Plus" then
                    local v65 = fn34(new4(0, tbl5.Size * 2, 0, tbl5.Thickness), v64)
                    tbl19[1] = v65
                    local v66 = fn34(new4(0, tbl5.Thickness, 0, tbl5.Size * 2), v64)
                    tbl19[2] = v66
                elseif tbl5.Style == "Dot" then
                    local Frame = new("Frame")
                    Frame.BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.Color
                    Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    Frame.AnchorPoint = new5(0.5, 0.5)
                    Frame.Size = new4(0, tbl5.Size, 0, tbl5.Size)
                    Frame.Position = v64
                    new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = new6(1, 0)
                    Frame.Parent = ScreenGui
                    tbl19[1] = Frame
                elseif tbl5.Style == "Circle" then
                    local Frame = new("Frame")
                    Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    Frame.AnchorPoint = new5(0.5, 0.5)
                    Frame.Size = new4(0, tbl5.Size * 2, 0, tbl5.Size * 2)
                    Frame.Position = v64
                    Frame.Parent = ScreenGui
                    local UIStroke = new("UIStroke")
                    UIStroke.Color = tbl5.Color
                    UIStroke.Thickness = tbl5.Thickness
                    UIStroke.Parent = Frame
                    new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = new6(1, 0)
                    tbl19[1] = Frame
                end
            end

            local v64 = new4(0.5, tbl5.OffsetX, 0.5, tbl5.OffsetY)

            if tbl5.Style == "Plus" then
                if tbl19[1] then
                    tbl19[1].Size = new4(0, tbl5.Size * 2, 0, tbl5.Thickness)
                    tbl19[1].Position = v64
                    tbl19[1].BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.Color
                end

                if tbl19[2] then
                    tbl19[2].Size = new4(0, tbl5.Thickness, 0, tbl5.Size * 2)
                    tbl19[2].Position = v64
                    tbl19[2].BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.Color
                end
            elseif tbl5.Style == "Dot" then
                if tbl19[1] then
                    tbl19[1].Size = new4(0, tbl5.Size, 0, tbl5.Size)
                    tbl19[1].Position = v64
                    tbl19[1].BackgroundColor3 = tbl5.Color
                end
            elseif tbl5.Style == "Circle" and tbl19[1] then
                tbl19[1].Size = new4(0, tbl5.Size * 2, 0, tbl5.Size * 2)
                tbl19[1].Position = v64
                local uiStroke = tbl19[1]:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

                if uiStroke then
                    uiStroke.Color = tbl5.Color
                    uiStroke.Thickness = tbl5.Thickness
                end
            end
        end

        local n3 = 0

        ScanMap = function()
            if v21() - n3 < 2 then
                return
            end
            n3 = v21()
            clear(tbl.Generators)
            clear(tbl.Windows)
            clear(tbl.Pallets)
            clear(tbl.Hooks)
            clear(tbl.Gates)
            clear(tbl.SCPs)
            local map = v2:FindFirstChild("Map")
            if not map then
                return
            end
            local descendants = map:GetDescendants()

            for i = 1, #descendants do
                if i % 300 == 0 then
                    wait()
                end

                local v64 = descendants[i]

                if v64:IsA("Model") then
                    local v65 = lower(v64.Name)

                    if v65 == "generator" then
                        local primaryPart = v64.PrimaryPart
                        local basePart = primaryPart or v64:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

                        if basePart then
                            insert(tbl.Generators, {model = v64, part = basePart})
                        end
                    elseif v65 == "hook" then
                        if v64:FindFirstChild("HookPoint") then
                            insert(tbl.Hooks, v64)
                        end
                    elseif v65 == "gate" then
                        if v64:FindFirstChild("ExitLever") then
                            insert(tbl.Gates, v64)
                        end
                    elseif v65 == "window" then
                        insert(tbl.Windows, v64)
                    elseif match(v65, "^scp") then
                        insert(tbl.SCPs, v64)
                    end
                elseif v64:IsA("BasePart") and v64.Name == "PrimaryPartPallet" then
                    local parent = v64.Parent

                    if parent and not find2(tbl.Pallets, parent) then
                        insert(tbl.Pallets, parent)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local items2 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Items", 5)

        extractAssetId = function(arg)
            return v59(arg):match("%d+")
        end

        getItemIcon = function(arg)
            if not items2 then
                return nil
            end
            local v64 = items2:FindFirstChild(arg)
            if not v64 then
                return nil
            end
            local v65 = nil

            v11(function()
                local texture = v64.Texture
                local image = texture or v64.Image
                v65 = image
            end)

            if v65 and v65 ~= "" then
                local v66 = extractAssetId(v65)
                if v66 then
                    return string.format("rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=%s&w=420&h=420", v66)
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        CreateModernESP = function(parent, name, arg)
            local v64 = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
            local v65 = v64

            if not v64 then
                local BillboardGui = new("BillboardGui")
                v65 = BillboardGui
                BillboardGui.Name = name
                BillboardGui.Parent = parent
                BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                BillboardGui.Size = new4(0, 200, 0, 25)
                local offsetY = arg.offsetY
                local n4 = offsetY or 3.5
                BillboardGui.StudsOffset = new2(0, n4, 0)
                local Frame = new("Frame")
                Frame.Name = "Box"
                Frame.AutomaticSize = automaticSize.X
                Frame.Size = new4(0, 0, 0, 15)
                Frame.Position = new4(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
                Frame.AnchorPoint = new5(0.5, 0)
                Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
                Frame.ZIndex = 2
                Frame.Parent = BillboardGui
                local UIListLayout = new("UIListLayout", Frame)
                UIListLayout.FillDirection = fillDirection.Horizontal
                UIListLayout.VerticalAlignment = verticalAlignment.Center
                UIListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = horizontalAlignment.Center
                UIListLayout.Padding = new6(0, 3)
                UIListLayout.SortOrder = sortOrder.LayoutOrder
                local ImageLabel = new("ImageLabel", Frame)
                ImageLabel.Name = "Icon"
                ImageLabel.Size = new4(0, 12, 0, 12)
                ImageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                ImageLabel.ZIndex = 3
                ImageLabel.LayoutOrder = 1
                ImageLabel.Visible = false
                local TextLabel = new("TextLabel", Frame)
                TextLabel.Name = "Text"
                TextLabel.AutomaticSize = automaticSize.X
                TextLabel.Size = new4(0, 0, 1, 0)
                TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                TextLabel.Font = font.GothamBlack
                TextLabel.TextSize = 11
                TextLabel.ZIndex = 3
                TextLabel.LayoutOrder = 2
                TextLabel.RichText = true
                TextLabel.TextXAlignment = textXAlignment.Center
                TextLabel.TextYAlignment = textYAlignment.Center
                TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                TextLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end

            local box = v65:FindFirstChild("Box")

            if box then
                box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                local uiGradient = box:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")

                if uiGradient then
                    uiGradient:Destroy()
                end

                local uiPadding = box:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIPadding")

                if uiPadding then
                    uiPadding:Destroy()
                end

                local uiCorner = box:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")

                if uiCorner then
                    uiCorner:Destroy()
                end
            end

            local line = v65:FindFirstChild("Line")

            if line then
                line.Visible = false
            end

            local icon = v65.Box:FindFirstChild("Icon")

            if arg.icon and arg.icon ~= "" then
                if icon then
                    icon.Image = arg.icon
                    icon.Visible = true
                end
            elseif icon then
                icon.Visible = false
            end

            local color = arg.color
            local v66 = color or fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            local v67 = format("#%02X%02X%02X", floor(v66.R * 255), floor(v66.G * 255), floor(v66.B * 255))

            if arg.distance then
                v65.Box.Text.Text = format("<font color='%s'><b>%s</b></font> <font color='#FFFFFF'>[%dm]</font>", v67, arg.name, arg.distance)
            elseif arg.subtext then
                v65.Box.Text.Text = format("<font color='%s'><b>%s</b></font> <font color='#FFFFFF'>%s</font>", v67, arg.name, arg.subtext)
            else
                v65.Box.Text.Text = format("<font color='%s'><b>%s</b></font>", v67, arg.name)
            end
        end

        ClearESP = function(arg)
            if arg == "Player" then
                for k, v64 in v6(fn()) do
                    if v64.Character then
                        if v64.Character:FindFirstChild("PEH") then
                            v64.Character.PEH:Destroy()
                        end

                        if v64.Character:FindFirstChild("PE_Text") then
                            v64.Character.PE_Text:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif arg == "Killer" then
                for k, v64 in v6(fn()) do
                    if v64.Character then
                        if v64.Character:FindFirstChild("KEH") then
                            v64.Character.KEH:Destroy()
                        end

                        if v64.Character:FindFirstChild("KE_Text") then
                            v64.Character.KE_Text:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif arg == "Generator" then
                for k, generator in v6(tbl.Generators) do
                    if generator.model and generator.model.Parent then
                        if generator.model:FindFirstChild("GEH") then
                            generator.model.GEH:Destroy()
                        end

                        if generator.model:FindFirstChild("GE_Text") then
                            generator.model.GE_Text:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif arg == "Pallet" then
                for k, pallet in v6(tbl.Pallets) do
                    if pallet and pallet.Parent and pallet:FindFirstChild("PalletEH") then
                        pallet.PalletEH:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            elseif arg == "Hook" then
                for k, hook in v6(tbl.Hooks) do
                    if hook and hook.Parent and hook:FindFirstChild("HookEH") then
                        hook.HookEH:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            elseif arg == "Window" then
                for k, window in v6(tbl.Windows) do
                    if window and window.Parent and window:FindFirstChild("WindowEH") then
                        window.WindowEH:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            elseif arg == "Gate" then
                for k, gate in v6(tbl.Gates) do
                    if gate and gate.Parent and gate:FindFirstChild("GateEH") then
                        gate.GateEH:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            elseif arg == "SCP" then
                for k, scP in v6(tbl.SCPs) do
                    if scP and scP.Parent and scP:FindFirstChild("SCPEH") then
                        scP.SCPEH:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        ClearAllESP = function()
            ClearESP("Player")
            ClearESP("Killer")
            ClearESP("Generator")
            ClearESP("Pallet")
            ClearESP("Hook")
            ClearESP("Gate")
            ClearESP("SCP")
            ClearESP("Window")
            ClearHookESP()
        end

        UpdatePlayerESP = function()
            if not tbl3.ESP_Master then
                return
            end
            local character = localPlayer2.Character
            local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if tbl3.ESP_KillerWarn and humanoidRootPart and GetRole() == "Survivor" then
                local killerWarn = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("KillerWarn")
                local v64 = killerWarn
                local huge = math.huge

                for k, v65 in v8(fn()) do
                    if v65 ~= localPlayer2 and IsKiller(v65) and v65.Character then
                        local humanoidRootPart2 = v65.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        if humanoidRootPart2 then
                            local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart2.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

                            if magnitude < huge then
                                huge = magnitude
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if huge <= 80 then
                    if not killerWarn then
                        local BillboardGui = new("BillboardGui")
                        v64 = BillboardGui
                        BillboardGui.Name = "KillerWarn"
                        BillboardGui.Size = new4(0, 30, 0, 30)
                        BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                        BillboardGui.StudsOffset = new2(0, 4, 0)
                        BillboardGui.Parent = humanoidRootPart
                        local TextLabel = new("TextLabel")
                        TextLabel.Name = "WarnText"
                        TextLabel.Size = new4(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        TextLabel.TextScaled = true
                        TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                        TextLabel.Font = font.GothamBlack
                        TextLabel.Parent = BillboardGui
                    end

                    local warnText = v64:FindFirstChild("WarnText")

                    if warnText then
                        if huge <= 40 then
                            warnText.Text = "!!"
                            warnText.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                        else
                            warnText.Text = "!"
                            warnText.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
                        end
                    end
                elseif killerWarn then
                    killerWarn:Destroy()
                end
            else
                local killerWarn = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("KillerWarn")

                if killerWarn then
                    killerWarn:Destroy()
                end
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_Player or tbl3.ESP_Killer then
                for k, v64 in v6(fn()) do
                    if v64 ~= localPlayer2 and v64.Character then
                        local v65 = IsKiller(v64)
                        local head = v64.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

                        if head and (not v65 and tbl3.ESP_Player or v65 and tbl3.ESP_Killer) then
                            local n4 = humanoidRootPart and floor((head.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                            local killer = v65 and tbl2.Colors.Killer or tbl2.Colors.Player
                            local name = v65 and "KEH" or "PEH"
                            local str = v65 and "KE_Text" or "PE_Text"
                            local v66 = v64.Character:FindFirstChild(name)
                            local v67 = v66

                            if not v66 then
                                local Highlight2 = new("Highlight")
                                v67 = Highlight2
                                Highlight2.Name = name
                                Highlight2.Parent = v64.Character
                            end

                            v67.FillColor = killer
                            v67.OutlineColor = killer
                            local espOutline = tbl3.ESP_Outline
                            local fillTransparency = espOutline and 1 or 0.5
                            v67.FillTransparency = fillTransparency
                            v67.OutlineTransparency = 0

                            if tbl3.ESP_Name then
                                local v68 = nil

                                if tbl3.ESP_ItemIcon and not v65 then
                                    local attribute = v64:GetAttribute("EquippedItem")

                                    if attribute and attribute ~= "" then
                                        v68 = getItemIcon(attribute)
                                    end
                                end

                                local v69 = CreateModernESP
                                local character2 = v64.Character
                                local tbl20 = {name = v64.Name}
                                local espDistance = tbl3.ESP_Distance
                                local v70 = espDistance and n4 or nil
                                tbl20.distance = v70
                                tbl20.color = killer
                                tbl20.icon = v68
                                v69(character2, str, tbl20)
                            else
                                local v68 = v64.Character:FindFirstChild(str)

                                if v68 then
                                    v68:Destroy()
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        UpdateSCPESP = function()
            if not tbl3.ESP_Master then
                return
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_SCP then
                for k, scP in v8(tbl.SCPs) do
                    if scP and scP.Parent then
                        local humanoid = scP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local humanoidRootPart = scP:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local torso = humanoidRootPart or scP:FindFirstChild("Torso") or scP.PrimaryPart

                        if humanoid and torso and humanoid.Health > 0 and floor(humanoid.WalkSpeed) == 9 then
                            local scp = tbl2.Colors.SCP
                            local scpeh = scP:FindFirstChild("SCPEH")
                            local v64 = scpeh

                            if not scpeh then
                                local Highlight2 = new("Highlight")
                                v64 = Highlight2
                                Highlight2.Name = "SCPEH"
                                Highlight2.Parent = scP
                            end

                            v64.FillColor = scp
                            v64.OutlineColor = scp
                            local espOutline = tbl3.ESP_Outline
                            local fillTransparency = espOutline and 1 or 0.5
                            v64.FillTransparency = fillTransparency
                            v64.OutlineTransparency = 0
                        else
                            local scpeh = scP:FindFirstChild("SCPEH")

                            if scpeh then
                                scpeh:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                ClearESP("SCP")
            end
        end

        UpdateStaticESP = function()
            if not tbl3.ESP_Master then
                return
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_Generator then
                for k, generator in v6(tbl.Generators) do
                    local model = generator.model

                    if model and model.Parent then
                        local attribute = model:GetAttribute("RepairProgress")
                        local n4 = attribute or 0
                        local v64 = floor(n4)
                        local generatorDone = v64 >= 100 and tbl2.Colors.GeneratorDone or tbl2.Colors.Generator
                        local geh = model:FindFirstChild("GEH")
                        local v65 = geh

                        if not geh then
                            local Highlight2 = new("Highlight")
                            v65 = Highlight2
                            Highlight2.Name = "GEH"
                            Highlight2.Parent = model
                        end

                        v65.FillColor = generatorDone
                        v65.OutlineColor = generatorDone
                        local espOutline = tbl3.ESP_Outline
                        local fillTransparency = espOutline and 1 or 0.5
                        v65.FillTransparency = fillTransparency
                        v65.OutlineTransparency = 0

                        if tbl3.ESP_GeneratorName then
                            local str = v64 >= 100 and "100%" or format("%d%%", v64)
                            CreateModernESP(model, "GE_Text", {name = "GEN", subtext = str, color = generatorDone, icon = nil})
                        else
                            local geText = model:FindFirstChild("GE_Text")

                            if geText then
                                geText:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_Pallet then
                for k, pallet in v6(tbl.Pallets) do
                    if pallet and pallet.Parent then
                        local palletEH = pallet:FindFirstChild("PalletEH")
                        local v64 = palletEH

                        if not palletEH then
                            local Highlight2 = new("Highlight")
                            v64 = Highlight2
                            Highlight2.Name = "PalletEH"
                            Highlight2.Parent = pallet
                        end

                        v64.Adornee = pallet
                        v64.FillColor = tbl2.Colors.Pallet
                        v64.OutlineColor = tbl2.Colors.Pallet
                        local espOutline = tbl3.ESP_Outline
                        local fillTransparency = espOutline and 1 or 0.5
                        v64.FillTransparency = fillTransparency
                        v64.OutlineTransparency = 0
                    end
                end
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_Window then
                for k, window in v8(tbl.Windows) do
                    if window and window.Parent then
                        local bottom = window:FindFirstChild("Bottom")
                        local basePart = bottom or window:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

                        if basePart then
                            local windowEH = window:FindFirstChild("WindowEH")
                            local v64 = windowEH

                            if not windowEH then
                                local BoxHandleAdornment = new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                                v64 = BoxHandleAdornment
                                BoxHandleAdornment.Name = "WindowEH"
                                BoxHandleAdornment.Parent = window
                                BoxHandleAdornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
                                BoxHandleAdornment.ZIndex = 5
                            end

                            v64.Adornee = basePart
                            v64.Size = basePart.Size
                            v64.Color3 = tbl2.Colors.Window
                            local espOutline = tbl3.ESP_Outline
                            local transparency = espOutline and 0.8 or 0.4
                            v64.Transparency = transparency
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_Hook then
                for k, hook in v8(tbl.Hooks) do
                    if hook and hook.Parent then
                        local hookEH = hook:FindFirstChild("HookEH")
                        local v64 = hookEH

                        if not hookEH then
                            local Highlight2 = new("Highlight")
                            v64 = Highlight2
                            Highlight2.Name = "HookEH"
                            Highlight2.Parent = hook
                        end

                        v64.FillColor = tbl2.Colors.Hook
                        v64.OutlineColor = tbl2.Colors.Hook
                        local espOutline = tbl3.ESP_Outline
                        local fillTransparency = espOutline and 1 or 0.5
                        v64.FillTransparency = fillTransparency
                        v64.OutlineTransparency = 0
                    end
                end
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_Gate then
                for k, gate in v8(tbl.Gates) do
                    if gate and gate.Parent then
                        local gateEH = gate:FindFirstChild("GateEH")
                        local v64 = gateEH

                        if not gateEH then
                            local Highlight2 = new("Highlight")
                            v64 = Highlight2
                            Highlight2.Name = "GateEH"
                            Highlight2.Parent = gate
                        end

                        v64.Adornee = gate

        getBestAimbotTarget = function()
            if v21() - n4 < 0.1 and v64 ~= nil and v64.Parent then
                return v64
            end
            local v65 = nil
            local v66 = GetRole()
            local survAimbotRadius = tbl3.Surv_Aimbot_Radius
            local huge = math.huge
            local v67 = new5(currentCamera.ViewportSize.X / 2, currentCamera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)

            for k, v68 in v6(fn()) do
                if v68 ~= localPlayer2 and v68.Character and v68.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local flag6 = false

                    if v66 == "Survivor" and tbl3.Surv_Aimbot_Enabled and IsKiller(v68) then
                        flag6 = true
                    elseif v66 == "Killer" and tbl3.Killer_Aimbot_Enabled and not IsKiller(v68) then
                        local humanoid = v68.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                        if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not IsDowned(v68.Character) then
                            flag6 = true
                        end
                    end

                    if flag6 then
                        local humanoidRootPart = v68.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                        local v69 = GetDistance(humanoidRootPart.Position)

                        if v66 == "Survivor" and v69 <= tbl3.Surv_Aimbot_MaxDist then
                            local v70, v71 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(humanoidRootPart.Position)

                            if v71 then
                                local magnitude = (new5(v70.X, v70.Y) - v67).Magnitude

                                if magnitude < survAimbotRadius then
                                    survAimbotRadius = magnitude
                                    v65 = humanoidRootPart
                                end
                            end
                        elseif v66 == "Killer" and v69 <= tbl3.Killer_Aimbot_MaxDist and v69 < huge then
                            huge = v69
                            v65 = humanoidRootPart
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            v64 = v65
            n4 = v21()
            return v65
        end

        PlayRepairAnim = function()
            local character = localPlayer2.Character
            if not character then
                return
            end
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if not animator then
                return
            end

            if RepairAnimTrack and RepairAnimTrack.IsPlaying then
                return
            end

            v11(function()
                local Animation = new("Animation")
                Animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://92960319113695"
                local v67 = animator:LoadAnimation(Animation)
                RepairAnimTrack = v67
                RepairAnimTrack.Priority = animationPriority.Action
                RepairAnimTrack:Play()
            end)
        end

        StopRepairAnim = function()

                v11(function()

                end)
            end

            RepairAnimTrack = nil
        end

        StopAutoRepair = function()
            StopRepairAnim()

                end)

        StartAutoRepairLoop = function(arg)
            AutoCurrentGenModel = arg

            if AutoRepairThread then
                cancel(AutoRepairThread)
                AutoRepairThread = nil
            end

            StopAutoRepair()

            local v67 = spawn(function()
                while AutoRepairEnabled and BypassGenEnabled do
                    local character = localPlayer2.Character
                    local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not humanoidRootPart then
                        wait(0.1)
                        continue
                    end
                    local v67 = nil
                    local v68, v69, v70 = v6(GetAllGenerators())
                    local v71 = v70
                    local v72 = v68
                    local v73 = v69
                    local v74, v75

                    repeat
                        v74, v75 = v72(v73, v71)
                        if v74 == nil then
                            break
                        end

                        for k, v76 in v6(GetGeneratorPoints(v75)) do
                            if (humanoidRootPart.Position - v76.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then
                                v67 = v76
                                break
                            end
                        end

                        v71 = v74
                    until v67

                    if v67 then
                        AutoCurrentPoint = v67
                        local v76 = v21()

                        if AutoFireInterval <= v76 - LastFireTime then
                            PlayRepairAnim()

                            v11(function()
                                repairEvent:FireServer(v67, true)
                            end)

                            LastFireTime = v76
                        end
                    elseif AutoCurrentPoint then
                        StopAutoRepair()
                        AutoRepairEnabled = false

                        if AutoCurrentGenModel then
                            ProcessedGens[AutoCurrentGenModel] = nil
                            AutoCurrentGenModel = nil
                        end

                        break
                    end

                    wait(0.1)
                end

                StopAutoRepair()
            end)

            AutoRepairThread = v67
        end

        waitForRepairing = function(arg, arg2)
            local v67 = v21()

            while true do
                local n6 = v21() - v67
                local n7 = arg2 or 1
                if n6 >= n7 then
                    break
                end

                if arg:GetAttribute("IsRepairing") == true then
                    return true
                end
                wait(0.05)
            end

            return false
        end

        DoMultiRepairPlain = function(arg)
            local parent = arg.Parent
            if ProcessedGens[parent] then
                return
            end
            ProcessedGens[parent] = true
            local v67 = GetGeneratorPoints(parent)
            local character = localPlayer2.Character
            local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart then
                ProcessedGens[parent] = nil
                return
            end
            local cFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame

            v11(function()
                for k, v68 in v6(v67) do
                    if v68 ~= arg and v68.Parent then
                        humanoidRootPart.Anchored = true
                        humanoidRootPart.CFrame = v68.CFrame
                        wait(0.15)

                        v11(function()
                            repairEvent:FireServer(v68, true)
                        end)

                        if not waitForRepairing(v68, 0.8) then
                            v11(function()
                                repairEvent:FireServer(v68, false)
                            end)

                            wait(0.1)
                            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = v68.CFrame
                            wait(0.15)

                            v11(function()
                                repairEvent:FireServer(v68, true)
                            end)

                            waitForRepairing(v68, 0.5)
                        end

                        humanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
                        wait(0.05)
                    end
                end
            end)

            v11(function()
                if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent then
                    humanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame
                end
            end)

            wait(0.1)

            if BypassGenMode == "Manual Repair" then
                v11(function()
                    repairEvent:FireServer(arg, false)
                end)
            elseif BypassGenMode == "Auto Repair" then
                AutoRepairEnabled = true
                StartAutoRepairLoop(parent)
            end
        end

                v71 = v74

            end
        end)

        tapMobileParryButton = function()

                            VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 1, true, v32, 0)
                            wait(0.01)
                            VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 1, false, v32, 0)
                            return
                        end

                        v17()
                        return
                    end

                    v3()
                end)
            end
        end

        ExecuteParry = function()
            if tbl10.ParryCooldown then
                return
            end

            v11(function()
                local parry = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes"):FindFirstChild("Items"):FindFirstChild("Parrying Dagger"):FindFirstChild("parry")

                if parry then
                    for i = 1, 10 do
                        parry:FireServer()
                    end
                end

                spawn(tapMobileParryButton)
            end)
        end

        ListenToParryResult = function()
            spawn(function()
                local remotes2 = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes", 5)
                local parryingDagger = remotes2 and remotes2:WaitForChild("Items", 5):WaitForChild("Parrying Dagger", 5)
                local parryResult = parryingDagger and parryingDagger:WaitForChild("parryResult", 5)

                if parryResult then
                    parryResult.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg, arg2)
                        local v67 = v22(arg2)
                        local n6 = v67 or (arg == true and 90 or 60)
                        tbl10.ParryCooldown = true

                        if tbl10.ParryCooldownThread then
                            cancel(tbl10.ParryCooldownThread)
                        end

                        local v68 = delay(n6, function()
                            tbl10.ParryCooldown = false
                        end)

                        tbl10.ParryCooldownThread = v68
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end

        ListenToParryResult()

        IsKillerFacingMe = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
            local humanoidRootPart = arg and arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart or not arg2 then
                return false
            end
            local v67 = v22(arg3)
            local n6 = v67 or 60
            local v68 = rad(n6)
            local n7 = arg2 - humanoidRootPart.Position
            if n7.Magnitude < 0.05 then
                return true
            end
            local v69 = new2(n7.X, 0, n7.Z)
            if v69.Magnitude < 0.05 then
                return true
            end
            local unit = v69.Unit
            local lookVector = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector
            local v70 = new2(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
            if v70.Magnitude <= 0.05 then
                return false
            end
            local v71 = v70.Unit:Dot(unit)
            local head = arg:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local v72 = v71

            if head then
                local lookVector2 = head.CFrame.LookVector
                local v73 = new2(lookVector2.X, 0, lookVector2.Z)

                if v73.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    v72 = v73.Unit:Dot(unit)
                end
            end

            local flag7 = cos(v68) <= v71 or cos(v68) <= v72
            return flag7
        end

        AttachParrySensor = function(arg)
            if not arg or tbl16[arg] then
                return
            end
            tbl16[arg] = true
            local humanoid = arg:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local v67 = humanoid

            if not humanoid then
                local humanoid2 = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                v67 = humanoid2
                if not humanoid2 then
                    return
                end
            end

            local animator = v67:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            local v68 = animator

            if not animator then
                local animator2 = arg:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                v68 = animator2
                if not animator2 then
                    return
                end
            end

            v67.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("Animator") then
                    tbl16[arg] = nil
                    AttachParrySensor(arg)
                end
            end)

            arg.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(child, parent)
                if not parent then
                    tbl16[arg] = nil
                end
            end)

            v68.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg2)
                local animation = arg2.Animation
                local animationId = animation and arg2.Animation.AnimationId or ""
                local match2 = animationId:match("%d+")
                local v69 = tbl15[match2]
                if not v69 then
                    return
                end

                if match2 == "80411309607666" and tbl3.Surv_AutoCrouch then
                    local character = localPlayer2.Character
                    if IsDowned(character) then
                        return
                    end
                    local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local humanoidRootPart2 = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart2 and (humanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude <= 40 then
                        TriggerCrouch()
                    end

                    return
                end

                if not tbl3.Surv_AutoParry then
                    return
                end

                if tbl10.ParryCooldown then
                    return
                end

                if tbl3.Ignored_Skills_List and tbl3.Ignored_Skills_List[v69] then
                    return
                end
                local character = localPlayer2.Character
                if IsDowned(character) or not IsSafeToParry(character) then
                    return
                end
                local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoidRootPart2 = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoidRootPart2 then
                    return
                end
                local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude

                if tbl3.Surv_ParryAntiFake ~= false then
                    local v70 = IsKillerFacingMe
                    local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
                    local survParryFacingAngle = tbl3.Surv_ParryFacingAngle
                    local n6 = survParryFacingAngle or 60
                    if not v70(arg, position, n6) then
                        return
                    end
                end

                if tbl3.Surv_ParryAggressive then
                    if tbl3.Surv_ParryRadius + 5 < magnitude then
                        return
                    end

                    if magnitude <= 12 then
                        ExecuteParry()
                    else
                        local connection = nil
                        local v70 = clock()

                        connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                            if clock() - v70 < 1.5 and not tbl10.ParryCooldown and humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart2 and not IsDowned(character) then
                                if n % 2 == 0 and (humanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude <= 12 then
                                    ExecuteParry()

                                    if connection then
                                        connection:Disconnect()
                                    end
                                end

                                return
                            end

                            if connection then
                                connection:Disconnect()
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                else
                    if tbl3.Surv_ParryRadius < magnitude then
                        return
                    end
                    local n6 = new2(humanoidRootPart.Position.X, 0, humanoidRootPart.Position.Z) - new2(humanoidRootPart2.Position.X, 0, humanoidRootPart2.Position.Z)

                    if n6.Magnitude > 0 then
                        local unit = n6.Unit
                        if new2(humanoidRootPart2.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, humanoidRootPart2.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit:Dot(unit) < tbl3.Surv_ParryFace then
                            return
                        end
                    end

                    ExecuteParry()
                end
            end)
        end

        TryAttach = function(arg)
            if arg ~= localPlayer and IsKiller(arg) and arg.Character then
                AttachParrySensor(arg.Character)
            end
        end

        SetupPlayer = function(arg)
            if arg == localPlayer then
                return
            end

            arg.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                TryAttach(arg)
            end)

            arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
                TryAttach(arg)
            end)

            if arg.Character then
                TryAttach(arg)
            end
        end

        local Lighting = v32:GetService("Lighting")
        local RunService2 = v32:GetService("RunService")

        local leenzPerf = {
            FullBright = false,
            NoFog = false,
            NoShadow = false,
            TimeOfDay = 14,
            GuardConn = nil,
            LowGFX = {
                Enabled = false,
                Textures = true,
                VisualEffects = true,
                Particles = true,
                Parts = true,
                Sky = false,
                Saved = {},
            },
        }

        v35.LeenzPerf = leenzPerf

        leenzPerf.ApplyFullBright = function()
            if not leenzPerf.FullBright then
                return
            end

            v11(function()
                Lighting.Brightness = 2.8
                Lighting.ClockTime = leenzPerf.TimeOfDay
                Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
                Lighting.Ambient = fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
            end)
        end

        leenzPerf.DisableFullBright = function()
            v11(function()
                Lighting.Brightness = 1
                Lighting.ClockTime = 14
                Lighting.FogEnd = 1000
                Lighting.Ambient = fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
            end)
        end

        leenzPerf.ApplyNoFog = function()
            if not leenzPerf.NoFog then
                return
            end

            v11(function()
                Lighting.FogStart = 0
                Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
                Lighting.FogColor = fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                local atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")

                if atmosphere then
                    atmosphere.Density = 0
                    atmosphere.Offset = 0
                    atmosphere.Glare = 0
                    atmosphere.Haze = 0
                end
            end)
        end

        leenzPerf.DisableNoFog = function()
            v11(function()
                Lighting.FogStart = 0
                Lighting.FogEnd = 1000
                local atmosphere = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere")

                if atmosphere then
                    atmosphere.Density = 0.35
                end
            end)
        end

        leenzPerf.ApplyNoShadow = function()
            v11(function()
                local globalShadows

                if leenzPerf.NoShadow then
                    globalShadows = false
                else
                    globalShadows = true
                end

                Lighting.GlobalShadows = globalShadows
            end)
        end

        local function fn34(arg, arg2, arg3)
            insert(leenzPerf.LowGFX.Saved, {obj = arg, kind = arg2, extra = arg3})
        end

        leenzPerf.LowGFX_ProcessOne = function(arg)
            if not leenzPerf.LowGFX.Enabled or not arg then
                return
            end
            local lowGFX = leenzPerf.LowGFX

            if lowGFX.Parts and (arg:IsA("BasePart") or arg:IsA("UnionOperation") or arg:IsA("MeshPart")) and arg.Material ~= material.SmoothPlastic then
                fn34(arg, "Part", arg.Material)
                arg.Material = material.SmoothPlastic
            end

            if lowGFX.Particles and (arg:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or arg:IsA("Smoke") or arg:IsA("Explosion") or arg:IsA("Sparkles") or arg:IsA("Fire")) and arg.Enabled ~= false then
                fn34(arg, "Particle", true)
                arg.Enabled = false
            end

            if lowGFX.VisualEffects and (arg:IsA("BloomEffect") or arg:IsA("BlurEffect") or arg:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or arg:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or arg:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")) and arg.Enabled ~= false then
                fn34(arg, "VFX", true)
                arg.Enabled = false
            end

            if lowGFX.Textures and (arg:IsA("Decal") or arg:IsA("Texture")) and arg.Texture ~= "" then
                fn34(arg, "Texture", arg.Texture)
                arg.Texture = ""
            end

            if lowGFX.Sky and arg:IsA("Sky") then
                fn34(arg, "Sky", arg.Parent)
                arg.Parent = nil
            end
        end

        leenzPerf.ApplyLowGFX = function()
            if not leenzPerf.LowGFX.Enabled then
                return
            end
            local v67, v68, v69 = v8({v2, Lighting})
            local v70 = v69
            local v71 = v67
            local v72 = v68

            while true do
                local v73, v74 = v71(v72, v70)
                if v73 == nil then
                    break
                end

                for k, descendant in v8(v74:GetDescendants()) do
                    if k % 250 == 0 then
                        wait()
                    end

                    leenzPerf.LowGFX_ProcessOne(descendant)
                end

                v70 = v73
            end
        end

        leenzPerf.RestoreLowGFX = function()
            for k, v67 in v8(leenzPerf.LowGFX.Saved) do
                local obj = v67.obj

                if obj and obj.Parent then
                    v11(function()
                        if v67.kind == "Part" then
                            obj.Material = v67.extra
                        elseif v67.kind ~= "Particle" and v67.kind ~= "VFX" then
                            if v67.kind == "Texture" then
                                obj.Texture = v67.extra
                            elseif v67.kind == "Sky" and v67.extra then
                                obj.Parent = v67.extra
                            end
                        else
                            obj.Enabled = v67.extra
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            clear(leenzPerf.LowGFX.Saved)
        end

        leenzPerf.ToggleLowGFX = function(enabled)
            leenzPerf.LowGFX.Enabled = enabled

            if enabled then
                spawn(leenzPerf.ApplyLowGFX)
            else
                leenzPerf.RestoreLowGFX()
            end
        end

        v2.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
            if not leenzPerf.LowGFX.Enabled then
                return
            end

            defer(function()
                leenzPerf.LowGFX_ProcessOne(descendant)
            end)
        end)

        leenzPerf.StartGuard = function()
            if leenzPerf.GuardConn then
                return
            end
            local n6 = 0

            leenzPerf.GuardConn = RunService2.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not leenzPerf.FullBright and not leenzPerf.NoFog and not leenzPerf.NoShadow then
                    return
                end
                local v67 = v21()
                if v67 - n6 < 0.4 then
                    return
                end
                n6 = v67

                if leenzPerf.FullBright then
                    leenzPerf.ApplyFullBright()
                end

                if leenzPerf.NoFog then
                    leenzPerf.ApplyNoFog()
                end

                if leenzPerf.NoShadow then
                    leenzPerf.ApplyNoShadow()
                end
            end)
        end

        leenzPerf.StopGuard = function()
            if leenzPerf.GuardConn then
                leenzPerf.GuardConn:Disconnect()
                leenzPerf.GuardConn = nil
            end
        end

        leenzPerf.SetFullBright = function(fullBright)
            leenzPerf.FullBright = fullBright

            if fullBright then
                leenzPerf.ApplyFullBright()
                leenzPerf.StartGuard()
            else
                leenzPerf.DisableFullBright()

                if not leenzPerf.NoFog and not leenzPerf.NoShadow then
                    leenzPerf.StopGuard()
                end
            end
        end

        leenzPerf.SetNoFog = function(noFog)
            leenzPerf.NoFog = noFog

            if noFog then
                leenzPerf.ApplyNoFog()
                leenzPerf.StartGuard()
            else
                leenzPerf.DisableNoFog()

                if not leenzPerf.FullBright and not leenzPerf.NoShadow then
                    leenzPerf.StopGuard()
                end
            end
        end

        leenzPerf.SetNoShadow = function(noShadow)
            leenzPerf.NoShadow = noShadow

            if noShadow then
                leenzPerf.StartGuard()
            elseif not leenzPerf.FullBright and not leenzPerf.NoFog then
                leenzPerf.StopGuard()
            end

            leenzPerf.ApplyNoShadow()
        end

        leenzPerf.SetTimeOfDay = function(timeOfDay)
            leenzPerf.TimeOfDay = timeOfDay

            if leenzPerf.FullBright then
                v11(function()
                    Lighting.ClockTime = timeOfDay
                end)
            end
        end

        v2.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "Map" then
                wait(1)

                if leenzPerf.FullBright then
                    leenzPerf.ApplyFullBright()
                end

                if leenzPerf.NoFog then
                    leenzPerf.ApplyNoFog()

                    spawn(leenzPerf.ApplyLowGFX)
                end
            end
        end)

        Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)

                    leenzPerf.ApplyNoFog()
                end)
            end

            if child:IsA("Sky") and leenzPerf.LowGFX.Enabled and leenzPerf.LowGFX.Sky then
                defer(function()
                    leenzPerf.LowGFX_ProcessOne(child)
                end)
            end
        end)

                        v87 = v80
                        v88 = v81
                        v89 = v86
                        n6 = 16
                    elseif n6 == 16 then
                        v90, v91 = v84(v85, v89)

                        if v90 == nil then
                            n6 = 19
                        else
                            n6 = 17
                        end
                    elseif n6 == 17 then
                        v89 = v90

                        if v4(v91) == "boolean" and v91 == true then
                            n6 = 18
                        else
                            n6 = 16
                        end
                    elseif n6 == 18 then
                        setupvalue(v83, v90, false)
                        n6 = 16
                    elseif n6 == 19 then
                        v79 = v82
                        v80 = v87
                        v81 = v88
                        n6 = 13
                    elseif n6 == 20 then
                        v23("Function tidak ditemukan.")

                    end

        PlayEmote = function()
            local character = localPlayer.Character
            if not character then
                return
            end
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoid or not humanoidRootPart then
                return
            end

            if currentTrack then
                currentTrack:Stop()
            end

            if currentSound then
                currentSound:Destroy()
            end

            if SelectedAnim then
                local Animation = new("Animation")
                Animation.AnimationId = SelectedAnim
                local v67 = humanoid:LoadAnimation(Animation)
                currentTrack = v67
                currentTrack.Looped = true
                currentTrack:Play()
            end

            if SelectedSound then
                local Sound2 = new("Sound")
                currentSound = Sound2
                currentSound.SoundId = SelectedSound
                currentSound.Looped = true
                currentSound.Volume = 2
                currentSound.Parent = humanoidRootPart
                currentSound:Play()
            end
        end

        StopEmote = function()
            if currentTrack then
                currentTrack:Stop()
            end

            if currentSound then
                currentSound:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local v67 = nil
        MoonwalkButtonVisible = false

        local function fn35()
            if v67 then
                v67:Disconnect()
                v67 = nil
            end

            local character = localPlayer2.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if humanoid then
                humanoid.AutoRotate = true
            end
        end

        local function fn36()
            fn35()
            local character = localPlayer2.Character
            local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
                return
            end
            humanoid.AutoRotate = false
            stateManager = true
        end

        local function fn37(arg)
            stateManager = arg

            if arg then
                fn36()
            else
                fn35()
            end
        end

        UpdateMoonwalkStatus = function()
            if not MoonwalkButton then
                return
            end
            local moonwalkLabel = MoonwalkButton:FindFirstChild("MoonwalkLabel")

            if MoonwalkEnabled then
                MoonwalkButton.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(0, 200, 100)
                MoonwalkButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1

                if moonwalkLabel then
                    moonwalkLabel.Text = "MOON"
                    moonwalkLabel.TextColor3 = fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
                end
            else
                MoonwalkButton.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
                MoonwalkButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15

                if moonwalkLabel then
                    moonwalkLabel.Text = "MOON"
                    moonwalkLabel.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                end
            end
        end

        ToggleMoonwalk = function()
            local flag7

            if MoonwalkEnabled then
                flag7 = false
            else
                flag7 = true
            end

            MoonwalkEnabled = flag7
            fn37(MoonwalkEnabled)
            UpdateMoonwalkStatus()
        end

        SetMoonwalkButtonVisible = function(visible)
            MoonwalkButtonVisible = visible

            if MoonwalkButton then
                MoonwalkButton.Visible = visible
            end

            if not visible and MoonwalkEnabled then
                MoonwalkEnabled = false
                fn37(false)
                UpdateMoonwalkStatus()
            end
        end

        UpdateMoonwalkVisibility = function()
            if MoonwalkButton then
                MoonwalkButton.Visible = MoonwalkEnabled
            end
        end

        gatherGates = function()
            local tbl21 = {}
            local map = v2:FindFirstChild("Map")
            if not map then
                return tbl21
            end

            for k, descendant in v6(map:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant.Name == "Gate" then
                    insert(tbl21, descendant)
                end
            end

            return tbl21
        end

        setBypassGate = function(arg)
            BypassGateEnabled = arg

            for k, v68 in v6(gatherGates()) do
                local leftGate = v68:FindFirstChild("LeftGate")
                local rightGate = v68:FindFirstChild("RightGate")
                local leftGateEnd = v68:FindFirstChild("LeftGate-end")
                local leftGateEnd2 = leftGateEnd or v68:FindFirstChild("LeftGate-end2")
                local rightGateEnd = v68:FindFirstChild("RightGate-end")
                local rightGateEnd2 = rightGateEnd or v68:FindFirstChild("RightGate-end2")
                local box = v68:FindFirstChild("Box")

                if arg then
                    if leftGate then
                        leftGate.Transparency = 1
                        leftGate.CanCollide = false
                    end

                    if rightGate then
                        rightGate.Transparency = 1
                        rightGate.CanCollide = false
                    end

                    if leftGateEnd2 then
                        leftGateEnd2.Transparency = 0
                    end

                    if rightGateEnd2 then
                        rightGateEnd2.Transparency = 0
                    end

                    if box then
                        box.CanCollide = false
                    end
                else
                    if leftGate then
                        leftGate.Transparency = 0
                        leftGate.CanCollide = true
                    end

                    if rightGate then
                        rightGate.Transparency = 0
                        rightGate.CanCollide = true
                    end

                    if leftGateEnd2 then
                        leftGateEnd2.Transparency = 1
                    end

                    if rightGateEnd2 then
                        rightGateEnd2.Transparency = 1
                    end

                    if box then
                        box.CanCollide = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        GetNearestKiller = function()
            local character = localPlayer.Character
            local humanoidRootPart = character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart then
                return nil, math.huge
            end
            local v68 = nil
            local huge = math.huge

            for k, v69 in v8(fn()) do
                if v69 ~= localPlayer and v69.Team and v69.Team.Name == "Killer" and v69.Character and v69.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local magnitude = (v69.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

                    if magnitude < huge then
                        huge = magnitude
                        v68 = v69
                    end
                end
            end

            return v68, huge
        end

        FleeKiller = function()
            if not FleeKillerEnabled then
                return
            end
            local character = localPlayer.Character
            local humanoidRootPart = character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart then
                return
            end
            local v68, v69 = GetNearestKiller()
            if not v68 or FleeDistance < v69 then
                return
            end

            if v21() - FleeCooldown < 3 then
                return
            end
            local character2 = v68.Character
            local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and v68.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not humanoidRootPart2 then
                return
            end
            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = new3(humanoidRootPart.Position + (humanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart2.Position).Unit * 80)
            local v70 = v21()
            FleeCooldown = v70
        end

        EnableInfiniteLunge = function()
            flag5 = true
            local character = localPlayer.Character

            if character then
                character:SetAttribute("lungeboost", 999)
            end
        end

        DisableInfiniteLunge = function()
            flag5 = false

            end
        end
