            end
        end)

        AutoPalletEnabled = false
        AutoPalletSafety = true
        AutoPalletDistance = 10
        local n6 = 0
        local tbl21 = {}
        local v68 = nil

        GetKillerRoot = function()
            for k, v69 in v8(fn()) do
                if v69 ~= localPlayer2 and (not v69.Team or v69.Team.Name ~= "Survivors") then
                    local character = v69.Character
                    if not character then
                        continue
                    end
                    local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if humanoidRootPart then
                        return humanoidRootPart
                    end
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        IsSafeToDropPallet = function(arg)
            if not AutoPalletSafety then
                return true
            end

            if not arg then
                return false
            end

            if arg:GetAttribute("Knocked") == true then
                return false
            end

            if arg:GetAttribute("IsCarried") == true then
                return false
            end

            if arg:GetAttribute("IsHooked") == true then
                return false
            end
            local checkInterractable = arg:FindFirstChild("CheckInterractable")
            if checkInterractable and (checkInterractable:GetAttribute("isVaulting") or checkInterractable:GetAttribute("isRepairing") or checkInterractable:GetAttribute("isUnhooking") or checkInterractable:GetAttribute("isHealing") or checkInterractable:GetAttribute("isSliding") or checkInterractable:GetAttribute("isCarrying")) then
                return false
            end
            local humanoid = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                return false
            end
            return true
        end

        FindNearestPallet = function(arg)
            local v69 = nil
            local v70 = AutoPalletDistance
            local pallets = tbl.Pallets
            local tbl22 = pallets or {}

            for k, v71 in v8(tbl22) do
                if v71 and v71.Parent and not tbl21[v71] then
                    local primaryPartPallet = v71:FindFirstChild("PrimaryPartPallet")
                    local palletPoint = primaryPartPallet or v71:FindFirstChild("PalletPoint") or v71:FindFirstChild("PalletPointSlide") or v71:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

                    if palletPoint then
                        local v72, v73 = v11(function()
                            return palletPoint.Position
                        end)

                        if v72 and v73 then
                            local magnitude = (arg.Position - v73).Magnitude

                            if magnitude < v70 then
                                v70 = magnitude
                                v69 = v71
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return v69
        end

        DropPallet = function(arg)
            if not arg then
                return false
            end
            local palletPointSlide = arg:FindFirstChild("PalletPointSlide")
            local palletPoint = palletPointSlide or arg:FindFirstChild("PalletPoint") or arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if not palletPoint then
                return false
            end
            local remotes2 = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local palletDropEvent = remotes2 and remotes2:FindFirstChild("Pallet") and remotes2.Pallet:FindFirstChild("PalletDropEvent")
            if not palletDropEvent then
                return false
            end

            v11(function()
                palletDropEvent:FireServer(palletPoint)
            end)

            tbl21[arg] = true
            n6 = v21()
            return true
        end

        StartAutoPallet = function()
            if v68 then
                return
            end

            v68 = spawn(function()
                while AutoPalletEnabled do
                    wait(0.4)

                    if GetRole() == "Survivor" and v21() - n6 >= 2.5 then
                        v11(function()
                            local character = localPlayer2.Character
                            local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                                return
                            end

                            if not IsSafeToDropPallet(character) then
                                return
                            end
                            local v69 = GetKillerRoot()
                            if not v69 then
                                return
                            end

                            if AutoPalletDistance < (humanoidRootPart.Position - v69.Position).Magnitude then
                                return
                            end
                            local v70 = FindNearestPallet(humanoidRootPart)

                            if v70 then
                                DropPallet(v70)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        StopAutoPallet = function()
            if v68 then
                cancel(v68)
                v68 = nil
            end
        end

        localPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            tbl21 = {}
            n6 = 0
        end)

        RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            n = n + 1

            if n > 1000000 then
                n = 0
            end

            fn33()
            local character = localPlayer.Character
            if not character then
                return
            end
            local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
                return
            end
            local v69 = v21()

            if tbl3.Killer_Aimbot_Enabled and isAimbotHolding and GetRole() == "Killer" and lockedAimbotTarget then
                local parent = lockedAimbotTarget.Parent
                local humanoid2 = parent and parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                if not humanoid2 or humanoid2.Health <= 0 or IsDowned(parent) then
                    local v70 = getBestAimbotTarget()
                    lockedAimbotTarget = v70
                end

                if lockedAimbotTarget then
                    local position = lockedAimbotTarget.Position
                    local v70 = GetDistance(position)

                    if tbl3.Killer_Aimbot_MaxDist < v70 then
                        local v71 = getBestAimbotTarget()
                        lockedAimbotTarget = v71
                    else
                        local v71 = new3(currentCamera.CFrame.Position, position)
                        currentCamera.CFrame = currentCamera.CFrame:Lerp(v71, tbl3.Killer_Aimbot_Smoothness)
                        local v72 = new3(humanoidRootPart.Position, new2(position.X, humanoidRootPart.Position.Y, position.Z))
                        humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame:Lerp(v72, tbl3.Killer_Aimbot_Smoothness)
                    end
                end
            end

            if isAimingFlash and tbl9.Flash_Silent then
                local v70 = getKillerTargetForFlash()

                if v70 then
                    local character2 = localPlayer2.Character
                    local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local n7 = v70.Position + new2(0, tbl9.Flash_YOffset, 0)
                    local currentCamera2 = v2.CurrentCamera
                    currentCamera2.CFrame = currentCamera2.CFrame:Lerp(lookAt(currentCamera2.CFrame.Position, n7), 0.5)

                    if humanoidRootPart2 then
                        local v71 = lookAt(humanoidRootPart2.Position, new2(n7.X, humanoidRootPart2.Position.Y, n7.Z))
                        humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = humanoidRootPart2.CFrame:Lerp(v71, 0.5)
                    end
                end
            end

            if n % 3 == 0 then
                local flag7 = character and character:GetAttribute("spearmode") == true

                if tbl9.Aim_SilentVeilV2 and tbl9.Veil_ShowFOV and flag7 then
                    Frame2.Visible = true
                    Frame2.Size = new4(0, tbl9.Veil_FOV * 2, 0, tbl9.Veil_FOV * 2)
                else
                    Frame2.Visible = false
                end

                if tbl9.Aim_SilentVeilV2 and flag7 then
                    v62 = getClosestSurvivor()

                    if v62 and v62.Parent then
                        Highlight.Parent = v62.Parent
                    else
                        Highlight.Parent = nil
                    end
                else
                    v62 = nil
                    Highlight.Parent = nil
                end
            end

            if tbl9.Aim_SilentVeilV2 and tbl9.Veil_ShowTracker then
                local flag7 = character:GetAttribute("spearmode") == true

                if flag7 and v62 and v62.Parent then
                    local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local v70 = head or humanoidRootPart

                    if v70 then
                        fn13(v70.Position, v62.Position)
                    end
                else
                    fn13(nil, nil)
                end
            else
                fn13(nil, nil)
            end

            if MoonwalkEnabled then
                local currentCamera2 = v2.CurrentCamera

                if currentCamera2 then
                    local lookVector = currentCamera2.CFrame.LookVector
                    local rightVector = currentCamera2.CFrame.RightVector
                    local v70 = new2(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
                    local unit = v70
                    local v71 = new2(rightVector.X, 0, rightVector.Z)
                    local unit2 = v71

                    if v70.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                        unit = v70.Unit
                    end

                    if v71.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                        unit2 = v71.Unit
                    end

                    local v72 = sin(v69 * 35)
                    local n7 = angles(0, rad(30 * v72), 0) * unit
                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = lookAt(humanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart.Position + n7)
                    humanoid:Move(-unit * 1.2 + unit2 * (v72 * 1.05), false)
                end
            end

            if n % 3 == 0 and tbl6.SkillCheck and not tbl10.busy then
                local skillCheckPromptGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("SkillCheckPromptGui")

                if skillCheckPromptGui then
                    local check = skillCheckPromptGui:FindFirstChild("Check")

                    if check and check.Visible then
                        local line = check:FindFirstChild("Line")
                        local goal = check:FindFirstChild("Goal")

                        if line and goal then
                            if tbl6.SkillCheckMode == "Instant" then
                                line.Rotation = goal.Rotation + 109
                                tbl10.busy = true

                                spawn(function()
                                    fn21()
                                    wait(0.05)
                                    tbl10.busy = false
                                end)
                            else
                                local n7 = line.Rotation % 360
                                local n8 = goal.Rotation % 360
                                local n9, n10

                                if tbl6.SkillCheckMode == "Normal" then
                                    n9 = (n8 + 116) % 360
                                    n10 = (n8 + 140) % 360
                                else
                                    n9 = (n8 + 102) % 360
                                    n10 = (n8 + 116) % 360
                                end

                                local v70

                                if n10 < n9 then
                                    local flag7 = n9 <= n7 or n7 <= n10
                                    v70 = flag7
                                else
                                    local flag7 = n9 <= n7 and n7 <= n10
                                    v70 = flag7
                                end

                                if v70 then
                                    tbl10.busy = true

                                    spawn(function()
                                        fn21()
                                        wait(0.05)
                                        tbl10.busy = false
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        local n7 = 0
        local n8 = 0
        local n9 = 0

        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local character = localPlayer.Character
            if not character then
                return
            end
            local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not humanoidRootPart or not humanoid then
                return
            end
            local v69 = v21()

            if n % 2 == 0 then
                if SpeedInputEnabled then
                    local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection

                    if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                        humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame + moveDirection * SpeedInputValue
                    end
                end

                if SpeedEnabled then
                    local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection

                    if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                        humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame + moveDirection * SpeedAmount
                    end
                end

                if GodMode and humanoid and humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
                    humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
                end

                if FleeKillerEnabled and v69 - n9 >= 0.3 then
                    n9 = v69
                    FleeKiller()
                end
            end

            if n % 5 == 0 then
                if tbl3.Surv_ParryCircle and tbl3.Surv_AutoParry and humanoidRootPart then
                    if not tbl10.AutoParryAdornment or tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Parent ~= humanoidRootPart then
                        if tbl10.AutoParryAdornment then
                            tbl10.AutoParryAdornment:Destroy()
                        end

                        local CylinderHandleAdornment = new("CylinderHandleAdornment")
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment = CylinderHandleAdornment
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Name = "AutoParryCircleESP"
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Height = 0.05
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Transparency = 0.3
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Adornee = humanoidRootPart
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Parent = humanoidRootPart
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.ZIndex = 0
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.AlwaysOnTop = false
                    end

                    local survParryRadius = tbl3.Surv_ParryRadius
                    tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Radius = survParryRadius
                    tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.InnerRadius = max(0.1, survParryRadius - 0.15)
                    tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.CFrame = new3(0, -3, 0) * angles(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0)

                    if tbl10.ParryCooldown then
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Color3 = fromRGB(255, 128, 0)
                    elseif tbl3.Surv_ParryAggressive then
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Color3 = fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                    else
                        tbl10.AutoParryAdornment.Color3 = fromRGB(0, 255, 255)
                    end
                elseif tbl10.AutoParryAdornment then
                    tbl10.AutoParryAdornment:Destroy()
                    tbl10.AutoParryAdornment = nil
                end
            end

            if NoSlowdownEnabled and v69 - n8 >= 0.3 then
                n8 = v69
                local humanoid2 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if humanoid2 and GetRole() == "Killer" and humanoid2.WalkSpeed < 16 then
                    humanoid2.WalkSpeed = 16
                end
            end

            if tbl3.ESP_Master and v69 - n7 >= 0.15 then
                n7 = v69

                if tbl3.ESP_Player or tbl3.ESP_Killer or tbl3.ESP_Name or tbl3.ESP_ItemIcon or tbl3.ESP_KillerWarn then
                    UpdatePlayerESP()
                end

                if tbl3.ESP_SCP then
                    UpdateSCPESP()
                end

                if tbl3.ESP_Generator or tbl3.ESP_Pallet or tbl3.ESP_Hook or tbl3.ESP_Gate or tbl3.ESP_Window then
                    UpdateStaticESP()
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local tbl13 = {Gui = nil, IsActive = false, GuiVisible = false}

    local function fn12(arg)
        if not tbl13.Gui then
            return
        end
        local frame = tbl13.Gui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if not frame then

        end

            if actionButton then

                actionButton.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                actionButton.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            end

            if uiStroke then

            end
        end
    end

        end)
    end

        if tbl13.Gui then
            tbl13.Gui:Destroy()

        TextButton.Text = "PARRY [OFF]"
        TextButton.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(30, 30, 35)
        TextButton.Font = font.GothamBold
        TextButton.TextSize = 12

        TextButton.BorderSizePixel = 0

        local flag5 = false

        local flag7 = false
        local position = nil
        local position2 = nil
        local v62 = nil

        local function fn15(arg)
            if not flag5 or not flag7 or not position or not position2 then
                return
            end
            local n2 = arg.Position - position

            if not flag6 and (abs(n2.X) > 18 or abs(n2.Y) > 18) then
                flag6 = true
            end

            if flag6 then
                Frame.Position = new4(position2.X.Scale, position2.X.Offset + n2.X, position2.Y.Scale, position2.Y.Offset + n2.Y)
            end
        end

        TextButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == userInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == userInputType.Touch then
                flag5 = true
                flag6 = false
                flag7 = false
                position = input.Position
                position2 = Frame.Position

                if v62 then
                    cancel(v62)
                    v62 = nil
                end

                v62 = delay(0.18, function()
                    if flag5 then
                        flag7 = true
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        TextButton.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == userInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == userInputType.Touch then
                if v62 then
                    cancel(v62)
                    v62 = nil
                end

                if flag5 and not flag6 then
                    fn13()
                end

                flag5 = false
                flag6 = false
                flag7 = false
                position = nil
                position2 = nil
            end
        end)

        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if flag5 and flag7 and (input.UserInputType == userInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == userInputType.Touch) then
                fn15(input)
            end
        end)

        if tbl13._statusConn then
            tbl13._statusConn:Disconnect()
        end

        tbl13._statusConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if tbl13.Gui and tbl13.Gui.Parent then
                if n % 20 ~= 0 then
                    return
                end

                if tbl13.IsActive ~= tbl3.Surv_AutoParry then
                    tbl13.IsActive = tbl3.Surv_AutoParry
                    fn12(tbl3.Surv_AutoParry)
                end

                return
            end

            if tbl13._statusConn then
                tbl13._statusConn:Disconnect()
                tbl13._statusConn = nil
            end
        end)
    end

    localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        wait(1)

    local function fn21(arg)

            return zero
        end

            if n3 > 0.001 and n3 < 0.1 then
                local v64 = v63.vel:Lerp((position - v63.pos) / n3, tbl16.LerpSmoothness)

        tbl15[arg] = tbl18

    end

    local function fn22(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
        local n3 = arg4 or 500
        local v63 = n3
        local position = arg2.Position
        if (position - arg).Magnitude < 8 then
            return position
        end
        local v64 = fn21(arg3)
        local n4 = position - arg
        local n5 = v64:Dot(v64) - v63 * v63
        local n6 = 2 * n4:Dot(v64)
        local v65 = n4:Dot(n4)
        local n7 = nil

        if abs(n5) < 0.01 then
            if abs(n6) > 0.01 then
                n7 = -v65 / n6
            end
        else
            local n8 = n6 * n6 - 4 * n5 * v65

            if n8 >= 0 then
                local v66 = sqrt(n8)
                local n9 = (-n6 - v66) / (2 * n5)
                local n10 = (-n6 + v66) / (2 * n5)

                if n9 > 0.001 then
                    n7 = n9
                elseif n10 > 0.001 then
                    n7 = n10
                end
            end
        end

        local n8 = position

        if n7 then
            local n9 = position + v64 * tbl16.PredictionEfficiency * n7
            n8 = n9

            if tbl16.EnableJitter then
                n8 = n9 + new2((random() - 0.5) * tbl16.MaxJitterStuds, (random() - 0.5) * (tbl16.MaxJitterStuds / 2), (random() - 0.5) * tbl16.MaxJitterStuds)
            end
        end

        return n8
    end

                return namecall2(arg, ...)
            end

            if flag3 then
                return namecall2(arg, ...)
            end
            local character = localPlayer2.Character
            local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local v67 = character

            if character then
                local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                v67 = head or humanoidRootPart
            end

            local v68, v69

            if flag17 then
                local spearAuraSpeed = tbl9.SPEAR_AuraSpeed
                local n3 = spearAuraSpeed or 165
                v68 = n3
                local spearAuraGravity = tbl9.SPEAR_AuraGravity
                local n4 = spearAuraGravity or 96.5
                v69 = n4
            else
                local spearSpeed = tbl9.SPEAR_Speed
                local n3 = spearSpeed or 165
                v68 = n3
                local spearGravity = tbl9.SPEAR_Gravity
                local n4 = spearGravity or 103
                v69 = n4
            end

            local value = (select(3, ...))

            if not (select(3, ...)) then
                local spearSmartEnable = tbl3.SpearSmart_enable
                value = spearSmartEnable and humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Position
            end

            if not value then
                value = v67 and v67.Position
            end

            local v70 = value
            local n3 = (...)
            local v71 = getClosestSurvivor()

            if v71 and v70 then
                local isModel = v71:IsA("Model")
                local humanoidRootPart2 = isModel and v71:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or v71
                local position = humanoidRootPart2.Position
                local n4 = new2(0, 0, 0)
                local parent = v71.Parent
                local humanoid = parent and v71.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if humanoid and humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                    n4 = humanoid.MoveDirection * humanoid.WalkSpeed
                elseif humanoidRootPart2:IsA("BasePart") then
                    n4 = humanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                end

                local v72 = new2(n4.X, 0, n4.Z)
                local n5 = position - v70
                local magnitude = new2(n5.X, 0, n5.Z).Magnitude
                local y = n5.Y

                if magnitude > 0.1 then
                    local v73, v74 = fn3(v68, v69, magnitude, y)

                    if tbl3.SpearSmart_enable and v72.Magnitude > 0.5 then
                        local veilLeadMultiplier = tbl9.Veil_LeadMultiplier
                        local n6 = veilLeadMultiplier or 1.4
                        local n7 = v72 * (v74 * n6)
                        local n8 = n7
                        local v75 = clamp(magnitude * 0.6, 3, 45)

                        if v75 < n7.Magnitude then
                            n8 = n7.Unit * v75
                        end

                        local n9 = position + n8 - v70
                        local magnitude2 = new2(n9.X, 0, n9.Z).Magnitude
                        local v76 = fn3(v68, v69, max(magnitude2, 0.1), n9.Y)

                        if magnitude2 > 0.001 then
                            n3 = new2(n9.X, 0, n9.Z).Unit * cos(v76) + new2(0, sin(v76), 0)
                        end
                    else
                        n3 = new2(n5.X, 0, n5.Z).Unit * cos(v73) + new2(0, sin(v73), 0)
                    end
                end
            end

            flag3 = true

            v11(function()
                if spearthrow then
                    spearthrow:FireServer(n3, v68, v70)
                else
                    arg:FireServer(n3, v68, v70)
                end
            end)

            flag3 = false
        end)

        v19(v65, true)
        flag2 = true
    end

        local v65 = spawn(function()
            while vd.KILLER_InfPursuit do
                v11(function()
                    local character = localPlayer2.Character

                    if character and character:GetAttribute("Pursuit") ~= true then
                        character:SetAttribute("Pursuit", true)
                    end
                end)

                wait(0.15)
            end

            InfPursuitThread = nil
        end)

        InfPursuitThread = v65
    end

    NEX_StopJasonPursuitBypass = function()
        v11(function()
            local character = localPlayer2.Character

            if character and character:GetAttribute("Pursuit") == true then
                character:SetAttribute("Pursuit", false)
                local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")

                if jason then

        end)
    end

    NEX_UpdateCureFlaskLaser = function()
        local character = v32:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then
            return
        end
        local position = nil
        local v65 = nil
        local v66 = nil
        local huge = math.huge
        local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart then
            local leftHand = character:FindFirstChild("LeftHand")
            local leftArm = leftHand or character:FindFirstChild("Left Arm")
            local position2 = leftArm and leftArm.Position or humanoidRootPart.Position
            v65 = position2

            for k, v67 in v6(fn()) do
                if v67 ~= v32:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer and v67.Character and v67.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and not v67.Character:GetAttribute("IsKiller") then
                    local magnitude = (v67.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

                    if magnitude < huge then
                        huge = magnitude
                        v66 = v67
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if v66 then
            position = v66.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        end

        local flag17 = false

        for k, child in v6(character:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("LocalScript") and child:GetAttribute("action") == true then
                flag17 = true
                break
            end
        end

        if v65 and position and flag17 then
            if not v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart then
                local Part3 = new("Part")
                Part3.Name = "FlaskSilentAimLaser"
                Part3.Anchored = true
                Part3.CanCollide = false
                Part3.CanTouch = false
                Part3.CastShadow = false
                Part3.Material = material.Neon
                Part3.Color = fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                Part3.Transparency = 0
                Part3.Parent = v2
                v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart = Part3
            end

            local magnitude = (position - v65).Magnitude

            if magnitude > 0.1 then
                local nexCureFlaskLaserPart = v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart
                nexCureFlaskLaserPart.Size = new2(0.16, 0.16, magnitude)
                nexCureFlaskLaserPart.CFrame = new3((v65 + position) / 2, position)
                nexCureFlaskLaserPart.Transparency = 0
            end
        elseif v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart then
            v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart.Transparency = 1
        end
    end

    NEX_StartCureFlaskLaser = function()
        if v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserThread then
            return
        end

        v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserThread = v32:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if n % 2 ~= 0 then
                return
            end

            if not vd.KILLER_FlaskLaser then
                if v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart then
                    v11(function()
                        v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart:Destroy()
                    end)

                    v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserPart = nil
                end

                if v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserThread then
                    v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserThread:Disconnect()
                    v51().NEX_CureFlaskLaserThread = nil
                end

                return
            end

            v11(NEX_UpdateCureFlaskLaser)
        end)
    end

    StartAntiLooping = function()
        local remotes = v32:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes
        local vaultEvent = remotes.Window.VaultEvent
        local palletSlideEvent = remotes.Pallet.PalletSlideEvent
        local map = v2:FindFirstChild("Map")

        if map then
            for k, descendant in v6(map:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant.Name == "VaultTrigger" then
                    v11(function()
                        vaultEvent:FireServer(descendant, true)
                    end)
                end

                if descendant.Name == "PalletPointSlide" and descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                    v11(function()
                        palletSlideEvent:FireServer(descendant, true)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        spawn(function()
            local map2 = v2:FindFirstChild("Map")
            if not map2 then
                return
            end
            local tbl20 = {}

            local function fn25(arg)
                if arg:IsA("BasePart") and arg.Name == "PalletPoint" then
                    insert(tbl20, arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Name"):Connect(function()
                        if not v35.BlockPalletEnabled then
                            return
                        end

                        if arg.Name == "PalletPointSlide" then
                            wait(0.1)

                            v11(function()
                                palletSlideEvent:FireServer(arg, true)
                            end)
                        end
                    end))
                end
            end

            for k, descendant in v6(map2:GetDescendants()) do
                fn25(descendant)
            end

            insert(tbl20, map2.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
                fn25(descendant)
            end))

            while v35.BlockPalletEnabled do
                wait(0.5)
            end

            for k, v65 in v6(tbl20) do
                v65:Disconnect()
            end
        end)
    end

                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    isWeapon = function(arg)
        if not arg or not arg:IsA("BasePart") then
            return false
        end
        local str2 = arg.Name:lower()
        local pos = str2:find("knife")
        local pos2 = pos or str2:find("sword") or str2:find("weapon") or str2:find("axe") or str2:find("bat") or str2:find("gun") or str2:find("spear") or str2:find("flask") or str2:find("stick")
        return pos2
    end

    ResetAllTransparency = function()
        local v67, v68, v69 = v6(currentCamera:GetChildren())
        local v70 = v69
        local v71 = v67
        local v72 = v68

        while true do
            local v73, v74 = v71(v72, v70)
            if v73 == nil then
                break
            end
            v70 = v73

            if v74 ~= localPlayer2.Character then
                for k, descendant in v6(v74:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Decal") then
                        v11(function()
                            descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                            descendant.Transparency = 0
                        end)
                    end
                end

                v70 = v73
            end
        end
    end

    end

    SetCharacterTransparency = function(arg, localTransparencyModifier)
        if not arg then
            return
        end
        local v67, v68, v69 = v6(arg:GetChildren())
        local v70 = v69
        local v71 = v67
        local v72 = v68

        while true do
            local v73, v74 = v71(v72, v70)
            if v73 == nil then
                break
            end
            v70 = v73

            if v74.Name == "Hat" then
                for k, descendant in v6(v74:GetDescendants()) do

                        v11(function()
                            descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = localTransparencyModifier
                            descendant.Transparency = localTransparencyModifier
                        end)
                    end
                end

                v70 = v73
            end

            if v74:IsA("BasePart") and v66 and v66[v74.Name] then
                v11(function()
                    v74.LocalTransparencyModifier = localTransparencyModifier
                    v74.Transparency = localTransparencyModifier
                end)
            end

            if isWeapon(v74) then
                local isBasePart = v74:IsA("BasePart")
                local descendants = isBasePart

                if isBasePart then
                    local tbl20 = {}
                    descendants = tbl20
                    tbl20[1] = v74
                end

                if not descendants then
                    descendants = v74:GetDescendants()
                end

                for k, descendant in v6(descendants) do
                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Decal") then
                        v11(function()
                            descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = localTransparencyModifier
                            descendant.Transparency = localTransparencyModifier
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    ResetCharacterTransparency = function(arg)
        if not localPlayer2.Character then
            return
        end

        if arg then
            local v67, v68, v69 = v6(localPlayer2.Character:GetChildren())
            local v70 = v69
            local v71 = v67
            local v72 = v68

            while true do
                local v73, v74 = v71(v72, v70)
                if v73 == nil then
                    break
                end
                v70 = v73

                if v74.Name == "Hat" then
                    for k, descendant in v6(v74:GetDescendants()) do
                        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Decal") then
                            v11(function()
                                descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                                descendant.Transparency = 1
                            end)
                        end
                    end

                    v70 = v73
                end

                if v74:IsA("BasePart") and v66 and v66[v74.Name] then
                    v11(function()
                        v74.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                        v74.Transparency = 1
                    end)
                end

                if isWeapon(v74) then
                    local isBasePart = v74:IsA("BasePart")
                    local descendants = isBasePart

                    if isBasePart then
                        local tbl20 = {}
                        descendants = tbl20
                        tbl20[1] = v74
                    end

                    if not descendants then
                        descendants = v74:GetDescendants()
                    end

                    for k, descendant in v6(descendants) do
                        if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Decal") then
                            v11(function()
                                descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                                descendant.Transparency = 1
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            SetCharacterTransparency(localPlayer2.Character, 0)
        end
    end

        WatermarkLabel.Text = "FebzTzy"
        WatermarkLabel.Parent = Frame
        local flag17 = false
        local position = nil
        local position2 = nil

        Frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == userInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == userInputType.Touch then
                flag17 = true
                position = input.Position
                position2 = Frame.Position
            end
        end)

        Frame.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == userInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == userInputType.Touch then
                flag17 = false
            end
        end)

        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if flag17 and (input.UserInputType == userInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == userInputType.Touch) then
                local n3 = input.Position - position
                Frame.Position = new4(position2.X.Scale, position2.X.Offset + n3.X, position2.Y.Scale, position2.Y.Offset + n3.Y)
            end
        end)
    end

    StartWatermark = function()
        if WatermarkConnection then

        end

            frame.Visible = true
        end

        local n4 = 60

        local connection7 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not WatermarkEnabled then
                if WatermarkConnection then
                    WatermarkConnection:Disconnect()
                    WatermarkConnection = nil
                end

                if WatermarkGui then
                    local frame2 = WatermarkGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")

                    if frame2 then
                        frame2.Visible = false
                    end
                end

                return
            end

            n3 = n3 + 1

            if v21() - v67 >= 1 then
                n4 = n3
                v67 = v21()
                n3 = 0
            end

            local n5 = 0

            v11(function()
                local value = v32:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
                local n6 = value or 0
                n5 = floor(n6)
            end)

            if WatermarkLabel then
                WatermarkLabel.Text = string.format("FebzTzy | %s fps | %s ms", floor(n4), n5)
            end
        end)

        WatermarkConnection = connection7
    end

    detectMap = function()
        local map = v2:FindFirstChild("Map")
        if not map then
            return nil
        end

        if map:FindFirstChild("random shakes") or map:FindFirstChild("SCP-173 Room") or map:FindFirstChild("SCP-205 Room") then
            return "Site 68"
        end

        if map:FindFirstChild("HooksMeat") then
            return "BLOODBATH! Club"
        end

        if map:FindFirstChild("Gate") and map.Gate:FindFirstChild("vfx") then
            return "Firelink Shrine"
        end

        if map:FindFirstChild("Bldg_Addon_RooftopUnit_A") or map:FindFirstChild("Rooftop") then
            return "Mercy Hospital Rooftop"
        end

        if map:FindFirstChild("White Armored Car") then
            return "Mount Massive Asylum"
        end

        if map:FindFirstChild("Dumbster") then
            return "The Bay Harbor"
        end

        if map:FindFirstChild("water pump") then
            return "Valdelobos Village"
        end

        if not map:FindFirstChild("LargeBoulder01") then
            return nil
        end
        return "Woodview Cabin"
    end

    StartMapPredict = function()
        spawn(function()
            local v67 = nil
            local flag17 = false

            while v51().MapPredictEnabled do
                local v68 = buildMapGui()
                local mainFrame = v68:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
                local enabled = false

                v11(function()
                    local team = localPlayer2.Team
                    local flag18 = team and localPlayer2.Team.Name == "Spectator"
                    enabled = flag18
                end)

                v68.Enabled = enabled

                if enabled and mainFrame then
                    local flag18 = v2:FindFirstChild("Map") ~= nil
                    local map = detectMap()

                    if flag17 and not flag18 then
                        local mapName = mainFrame.MapName
                        local map2 = v67 or "Unknown"
                        mapName.Text = "Map: " .. map2
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.Text = "Status: Setting up..."
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
                    elseif flag18 and map then
                        v67 = map
                        mainFrame.MapName.Text = "Map: " .. map
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.Text = "Status: Lobby"
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.TextColor3 = fromRGB(100, 255, 100)
                    elseif flag18 and not map then
                        mainFrame.MapName.Text = "Map: Unknown"
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.Text = "Status: Lobby"
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.TextColor3 = fromRGB(100, 255, 100)
                    else
                        mainFrame.MapName.Text = "Map: —"
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.Text = "Status: Lobby"
                        mainFrame.MapStatus.TextColor3 = fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
                    end

                    flag17 = flag18
                end

                wait(0.5)
            end

            cleanMapGui()
        end)
    end

    ShowStun = false
    StunData = {}

    SetupStun = function(arg)
        if not arg or StunData[arg] then
            return
        end
        local humanoidRootPart = arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoidRootPart then
            return
        end
        local stunUI = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("StunUI")

        if stunUI then
            stunUI:Destroy()
        end

        local BillboardGui = new("BillboardGui")
        BillboardGui.Name = "StunUI"
        BillboardGui.Adornee = humanoidRootPart
        BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
        BillboardGui.Size = new4(0, 150, 0, 40)
        BillboardGui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = new2(0, 4.5, 0)
        BillboardGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        BillboardGui.Parent = humanoidRootPart
        BillboardGui.Enabled = false
        local TextLabel = new("TextLabel", BillboardGui)
        TextLabel.Size = new4(1, 0, 0, 18)
        TextLabel.Position = new4(0, 0, 0, 0)
        TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        TextLabel.Text = "⚡ STUNNED"
        TextLabel.TextColor3 = fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        TextLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        TextLabel.TextScaled = true
        TextLabel.Font = font.GothamBlack
        local Frame = new("Frame", BillboardGui)
        Frame.Size = new4(1, 0, 0, 6)
        Frame.Position = new4(0, 0, 0, 22)
        Frame.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
        Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = new6(0, 3)
        local Frame2 = new("Frame", Frame)
        Frame2.Size = new4(1, 0, 1, 0)
        Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
        new("UICorner", Frame2).CornerRadius = new6(0, 3)

        StunData[arg] = {
            bill = BillboardGui,
            fill = Frame2,
            timer = nil,
            duration = 2.2,
            startTime = 0,
        }

        local function fn25()
            if tbl3.HitSoundEnabled then
                PlayHitSound()
            end

            local v67 = StunData[arg]
            if not v67 then
                return
            end
            v67.bill.Enabled = true
            v67.duration = 2.2
            v67.startTime = v21()

            if v67.timer then
                cancel(v67.timer)
            end

            v67.timer = spawn(function()
                while v67.bill and v67.bill.Enabled do
                    local n3 = 1 - (v21() - v67.startTime) / v67.duration
                    if n3 <= 0 then
                        v67.bill.Enabled = false
                        break
                    end
                    v67.fill.Size = new4(n3, 0, 1, 0)

                    if n3 > 0.5 then
                        v67.fill.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    elseif n3 > 0.2 then
                        v67.fill.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
                    else
                        v67.fill.BackgroundColor3 = fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
                    end

                    wait(0.05)
                end
            end)
        end

        arg:GetAttributeChangedSignal("IsStunned"):Connect(function()
            if arg:GetAttribute("IsStunned") == true then
                fn25()
            end
        end)

        arg:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Immobile"):Connect(function()
            if arg:GetAttribute("Immobile") == true then
                fn25()
            end
        end)
    end

    CleanupStun = function(arg)
        local v67 = StunData[arg]

        if v67 then
            if v67.timer then
                cancel(v67.timer)
            end

            if v67.bill then
                v67.bill:Destroy()
            end

            StunData[arg] = nil
        end
    end

    SetupAllStun = function()
        if not ShowStun then
            return
        end

        for k, v67 in v8(fn()) do
            if IsKiller(v67) and v67.Character then
                SetupStun(v67.Character)
            end
        end
    end

    UpdateSpectatorCount = function()
        if not spectatorEnabled or not SpectatorLabel then
            return

        local mainBox = v67 and SpectatorGui:FindFirstChild("MainBox")

        if mainBox then
            local n4 = n3 >= 10 and 8 or 0
            local n5 = 55 + n4
            local n6 = n3 >= 100 and 8 or 0
            mainBox.Size = new4(0, n5 + n6, 0, 22)
        end
    end

    KillerPerksEnabled = false

        if KillerPerksGui then

                KillerPerksGui:Destroy()

        if not KillerPerksEnabled then
            return
        end

        local function fn25(arg)
            local str2 = arg or ""
            return v59(str2):gsub("&", "&amp;"):gsub("<", "&lt;"):gsub(">", "&gt;")
        end

        local function fn26(arg)
            local str2 = arg or ""
            local match2, v67 = v59(str2):match("^(.+)%s+(%d+)$")
            if not match2 then
                return nil
            end
            local str3 = match2:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
            if str3 == "" then
                return nil
            end

            if not ({
                head = true,
                torso = true,
                humanoid = true,
                humanoidrootpart = true,
                ["left arm"] = true,
                ["right arm"] = true,
                ["left leg"] = true,
                ["right leg"] = true,
            })[str3:lower()] then
                return str3, v67
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function fn27()
            for k, v67 in v8(fn()) do
                local team = v67.Team
                local name = team and v67.Team.Name
                if name and name:lower():find("killer") then
                    return v67
                end
            end

            return nil
        end

        local function fn28(arg)
            if not arg then
                return {}
            end
            local character = arg.Character
            local v67 = character or v2:FindFirstChild(arg.Name) or v2:FindFirstChild(arg.DisplayName)
            if not v67 then
                return {}
            end
            local tbl20 = {}
            local tbl21 = {}

            for k, child in v8(v67:GetChildren()) do
                local v68, v69 = fn26(child.Name)

                if v68 and not tbl21[v68] then
                    tbl21[v68] = true
                    insert(tbl20, {Name = v68, Level = v69})
                end
            end

            sort(tbl20, function(arg2, arg3)
                local flag17 = v59(arg2.Name) < v59(arg3.Name)
                return flag17
            end)

            return tbl20
        end

        local function fn29()
            local v67 = fn27()
            local name = v67

            if v67 then
                local displayName = v67.DisplayName
                name = displayName or v67.Name
            end

            if not name then
                name = "Unknown"
            end

            local v68 = fn28(v67)
            local v69 = v68

            if #v68 == 0 then
                for k, v70 in v8(fn()) do
                    local v71 = fn28(v70)

                    if #v71 > 0 then
                        local displayName = v70.DisplayName
                        local name2 = displayName or v70.Name
                        name = name2
                        v69 = v71
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            local tbl20 = {
                "Killer Perks [<font color=\"rgb(255,80,80)\">" .. fn25(name) .. "</font>]",
            }

            if #v69 == 0 then
                insert(tbl20, "<font color=\"rgb(200,200,200)\">- Waiting for perk data...</font>")
            else
                for i = 1, min(#v69, 4) do
                    local v70 = v69[i]
                    local level = v70.Level
                    local str2 = level and " lvl " .. v59(v70.Level) or ""
                    insert(tbl20, "<font color=\"rgb(200,200,200)\">- " .. fn25(v70.Name .. str2) .. "</font>")
                end
            end

            return concat(tbl20, "\n"), #v69
        end

    TeleportToPart = function(arg)
        if not arg then
            return
        end
        local character = localPlayer.Character
        local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoidRootPart then
            local v67 = new2(0, 3, 0)

            if arg:IsA("BasePart") then
                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = arg.CFrame + v67
            elseif arg:IsA("Model") then
                local basePart = arg:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

                if basePart then
                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = basePart.CFrame + v67
                end
            end
        end
    end

    TeleportToGenerator = function()
        if #tbl.Generators == 0 then
            ScanMap()
        end

        if #tbl.Generators < genIndex then
            genIndex = 1
        end

        local v67 = tbl.Generators[genIndex]

        if v67 and v67.part then
            TeleportToPart(v67.part)
        else
            v23("Generator tidak ditemukan!")
        end

        genIndex = genIndex + 1
    end

    TeleportToHook = function()
        if #tbl.Hooks == 0 then
            ScanMap()
        end

        if #tbl.Hooks < hookIndex then
            hookIndex = 1
        end

        local v67 = tbl.Hooks[hookIndex]
        local basePart = v67

        if v67 then
            local hookPoint = v67:FindFirstChild("HookPoint")
            basePart = hookPoint or v67:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        end

        if basePart then
            TeleportToPart(basePart)
        end

        hookIndex = hookIndex + 1
    end

    TeleportToGate = function()
        if #tbl.Gates == 0 then
            ScanMap()
        end

        if #tbl.Gates < gateIndex then
            gateIndex = 1
        end

        local v67 = tbl.Gates[gateIndex]
        local basePart = v67 and v67:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

        if basePart then
            TeleportToPart(basePart)
        end

        gateIndex = gateIndex + 1
    end

    TeleportToPallet = function()
        if #tbl.Pallets == 0 then
            ScanMap()
        end

        if #tbl.Pallets < palletIndex then
            palletIndex = 1
        end

        local v67 = tbl.Pallets[palletIndex]

        end

        palletIndex = palletIndex + 1
    end

    TeleportToWindow = function()
        if #tbl.Windows == 0 then

        end

        if #tbl.Windows < windowIndex then

    end

    spawn(function()
        while true do
            wait(0.15)
            if UserInputService.TouchEnabled or v21() - lastActionTime < COOLDOWN then
                continue
            end
            local v67 = autoVaultEnabled
            local v68 = autoPalletSlideEnabled
            if not v67 and not v68 then
                continue
            end
            local pcprompts = playerGui:FindFirstChild("pcprompts")

                continue
            end

            local palletSlidePromptGui = frame:FindFirstChild("PalletSlidePromptGui")
            local visible = vaultPromptGui and vaultPromptGui.Visible
            local visible2 = palletSlidePromptGui and palletSlidePromptGui.Visible
            if (not v67 or not visible) and (not v68 or not visible2) then
                continue
            end
            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, keyCode.Space, false, v32)
            wait(0.01)
            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, keyCode.Space, false, v32)
            local v69 = v21()
            lastActionTime = v69
        end
    end)

    local function fn25()
        if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
            return
        end
        local survivorMob = playerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        local survivorMob2 = survivorMob or playerGui:WaitForChild("Survivor-mob", 8)
        if not survivorMob2 then
            return
        end
        local controls = survivorMob2:FindFirstChild("Controls")
        local controls2 = controls or survivorMob2:WaitForChild("Controls", 5)
        local action = controls2

        if controls2 then
            local action2 = controls2:FindFirstChild("action")
            action = action2 or controls2:WaitForChild("action", 5)
        end

        if not action then
            return
        end

        local tbl20 = {
            vault = true,
            palletvault = true,
            palletslide = true,
            slide = true,
        }

        local function fn26(arg)
            if not arg:IsA("ImageLabel") and not arg:IsA("ImageButton") then
                return
            end
            local str2 = arg.Name:lower()
            if not tbl20[str2] then
                return
            end

            arg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                if not arg.Visible then
                    return
                end

                if v21() - lastActionTime < COOLDOWN then
                    return
                end
                local pos = str2:find("vault")
                local flag17 = pos

                if pos then
                    if str2:find("pallet") then
                        flag17 = false
                    else
                        flag17 = true
                    end
                end

                local pos2 = str2:find("pallet")
                local pos3 = pos2 or str2:find("slide")

                if flag17 and autoVaultEnabled or pos3 and autoPalletSlideEnabled then
                    v11(function()
                        v56(action.MouseButton1Down)
                        wait(0.01)
                        v56(action.MouseButton1Up)
                    end)

                    local v67 = v21()
                    lastActionTime = v67
                end
            end)
        end

        for k, child in v6(action:GetChildren()) do
            fn26(child)
        end

        action.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            wait()
            fn26(child)
        end)
    end

    local function fn26(arg, arg2)
        if not arg or not arg.Team or not arg.Team.Name then
            return false
        end
        local v76 = lower(arg.Team.Name)
        if arg2 == "Killer" then

            return flag17

        if arg2 ~= "Survivor" then

        return flag17
    end

    if not W.GB_GetAllGenerators then
        W.GB_GetAllGenerators = function()
            local tbl22 = {}
            local map = v2:FindFirstChild("Map")

            if map then
                for k, descendant in v8(map:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("Model") and descendant.Name == "Generator" then
                        local flag17 = descendant:GetAttribute("RepairProgress") ~= nil or descendant:GetAttribute("kickcount") ~= nil or descendant:GetAttribute("ProgressRepair") ~= nil

                        end

                end
            end

            return tbl22
        end
    end

    W.SU_IsHooked = function()
        local character = localPlayer2.Character
        if not character then
            return false
        end
        local flag17 = character:GetAttribute("IsHooked") == true or character:GetAttribute("isHooked") == true or character:GetAttribute("Hooked") == true or character:GetAttribute("HookedState") == true
        return flag17
    end

    W.SU_GetRandomGeneratorPoint = function()
        local v76 = W.GB_GetAllGenerators()
        if not v76 or #v76 == 0 then
            return nil
        end
        local tbl22 = {}
        local v77, v78, v79 = v8(v76)
        local v80 = v79
        local v81 = v77
        local v82 = v78

        while true do
            local v83, v84 = v81(v82, v80)
            if v83 == nil then
                break
            end
            v80 = v83

            if v84 and v84.Parent then
                for k, v85 in v8(W.GB_GetPoints(v84)) do
                    if v85 and v85.Parent then
                        insert(tbl22, v85)
                    end
                end

                v80 = v83
            end
        end

        if #tbl22 == 0 then
            return nil
        end
        return tbl22[random(1, #tbl22)]
    end

    W.SU_GetKillerHRP = function()
        for k, v76 in v8(fn()) do
            if v76 ~= localPlayer2 and fn26(v76, "Killer") and v76.Character then
                local humanoidRootPart = v76.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if humanoidRootPart then

            end
        end

        return nil
    end

    W.SU_Abort = function()
        selfUnhook2.Following = false

        if selfUnhook2._activeThread and status(selfUnhook2._activeThread) ~= "dead" then
            v11(function()
                cancel(selfUnhook2._activeThread)
            end)
        end

        selfUnhook2._activeThread = nil
    end

    W.SU_Trigger = function()
        if selfUnhook2.Following then
            return
        end
        local v76 = v21()
        if v76 - selfUnhook2._lastTrigger < selfUnhook2._cooldown then
            return
        end

        if not W.SU_IsHooked() then
            return
        end
        selfUnhook2._lastTrigger = v76
        selfUnhook2.Following = true
        selfUnhook2.TriggerCount = selfUnhook2.TriggerCount + 1
        local character = localPlayer2.Character

        if character then
            local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if humanoidRootPart then
                selfUnhook2._hookPos = humanoidRootPart.Position
                selfUnhook2._hookCFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
            end
        end

        selfUnhook2._activeThread = spawn(function()
            local character2 = localPlayer2.Character

            if not character2 then
                selfUnhook2.Following = false
                selfUnhook2._activeThread = nil
                return
            end

            local humanoidRootPart = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if not humanoidRootPart then
                selfUnhook2.Following = false
                selfUnhook2._activeThread = nil
                return
            end

            local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local repairEvent = remotes and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Generator") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Generator:FindFirstChild("RepairEvent")

            if not W.SU_IsHooked() then
                selfUnhook2.Following = false
                selfUnhook2._activeThread = nil
                return
            end

            local v77 = W.SU_GetRandomGeneratorPoint()

            if v77 then
                v11(function()
                    humanoidRootPart.Velocity = zero
                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = v77.CFrame + new2(0, 3, 0)
                end)

                wait(0.15)

                if repairEvent then
                    v11(function()
                        repairEvent:FireServer(v77, true)
                    end)

                    wait(0.35)

                    v11(function()
                        repairEvent:FireServer(v77, false)
                    end)

                    wait(0.15)

                    v11(function()
                        repairEvent:FireServer(v77, true)
                    end)

                    wait(0.35)

                    v11(function()
                        repairEvent:FireServer(v77, false)
                    end)
                end
            end

            local v78 = v21()
            local followDuration = selfUnhook2.FollowDuration
            local n3 = followDuration or 30
            local n4 = v78 + n3
            local followDistance = selfUnhook2.FollowDistance
            local n5 = followDistance or 20

            while v21() < n4 and selfUnhook2.Enabled do
                if not W.SU_IsHooked() then
                    selfUnhook2.Following = false
                    selfUnhook2._activeThread = nil
                    return
                end

                local character3 = localPlayer2.Character
                if not character3 then
                    break
                end
                local humanoidRootPart2 = character3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoidRootPart2 then
                    break
                end
                local v79 = W.SU_GetKillerHRP()

                if v79 then
                    v11(function()
                        humanoidRootPart2.Velocity = zero
                        local v80 = new2(v79.Position.X, v79.Position.Y - n5, v79.Position.Z)
                        humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = new3(v80, v80 + new2(0, 0, -1))
                    end)
                end

                wait(0.05)
            end

            if W.SU_IsHooked() then
                local character3 = localPlayer2.Character

                if character3 then
                    local humanoidRootPart2 = character3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if humanoidRootPart2 then
                        if selfUnhook2._hookCFrame then
                            v11(function()
                                humanoidRootPart2.Velocity = zero
                                humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = selfUnhook2._hookCFrame
                            end)
                        elseif selfUnhook2._hookPos then
                            v11(function()
                                humanoidRootPart2.Velocity = zero
                                humanoidRootPart2.CFrame = new3(selfUnhook2._hookPos + new2(0, 3, 0))
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            selfUnhook2.Following = false
            selfUnhook2._activeThread = nil
        end)
    end

    W.SU_Start = function()
        if selfUnhook2.MonitorConn then
            return
        end
        selfUnhook2._wasHooked = false

        selfUnhook2.MonitorConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not selfUnhook2.Enabled then
                return
            end

            if n % 5 ~= 0 then
                return
            end

            if not localPlayer2.Character then
                return
            end

            if not W.SU_IsHooked() then
                selfUnhook2._wasHooked = false
                return
            end

            if not selfUnhook2._wasHooked and not selfUnhook2.Following then
                selfUnhook2._wasHooked = true
                W.SU_Trigger()
            end
        end)
    end

    W.SU_Stop = function()
        if selfUnhook2.MonitorConn then
            selfUnhook2.MonitorConn:Disconnect()
            selfUnhook2.MonitorConn = nil
        end

        W.SU_Abort()
        selfUnhook2._wasHooked = false
    end

    tbl20.KillerThirdPerson = v80:AddToggle({
        Name = "Third Person (Killer)",
        Default = false,
        ConfigKey = "killer_3rdperson",
        Callback = function(killer3rdPerson)
            local team = localPlayer.Team
            local flag17 = team and localPlayer.Team.Name == "Killer"

            if killer3rdPerson and not flag17 then
                if tbl20.KillerThirdPerson then
                    tbl20.KillerThirdPerson.Set(false)
                end

                return
            end

            tbl3.Killer_3rdPerson = killer3rdPerson

            if killer3rdPerson then
                localPlayer.CameraMode = cameraMode.Classic
                localPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 7
                localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 128

                if tbl10.ThirdPersonConn then
                    tbl10.ThirdPersonConn:Disconnect()
                    tbl10.ThirdPersonConn = nil
                end

                local connection7 = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    if n % 2 ~= 0 then
                        return
                    end

                    if not tbl3.Killer_3rdPerson then
                        return
                    end

                    if localPlayer.Team and localPlayer.Team.Name ~= "Killer" then
                        tbl3.Killer_3rdPerson = false

                        if tbl10.ThirdPersonConn then
                            tbl10.ThirdPersonConn:Disconnect()
                            tbl10.ThirdPersonConn = nil
                        end

                        return
                    end

                    localPlayer.CameraMode = cameraMode.Classic
                    localPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 7
                    localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 128
                    SetWorldTransparency(1)

                    if localPlayer.Character then
                        SetCharacterTransparency(localPlayer.Character, 0)
                    end
                end)

                tbl10.ThirdPersonConn = connection7
            else
                if tbl10.ThirdPersonConn then
                    tbl10.ThirdPersonConn:Disconnect()
                    tbl10.ThirdPersonConn = nil
                end

                if flag17 then
                    localPlayer.CameraMode = cameraMode.LockFirstPerson
                    localPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
                    localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 0.5
                else
                    localPlayer.CameraMode = cameraMode.Classic
                    localPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0.5
                    localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 128
                end

                ResetAllTransparency()

                if localPlayer.Character then
                    ResetCharacterTransparency(false)
                end
            end
        end,
    })

    local v86 = v82:CreateSection({Name = "Auto Pallet", Side = "Left"})

    v86:AddButton({

        Callback = function()
            local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local palletDropEvent = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Pallet") and remotes.Pallet:FindFirstChild("PalletDropEvent")
            local tbl23 = {};

            (function()
                tbl23 = {}
                local map = v2:FindFirstChild("Map")
                if not map then
                    return
                end

                for k, descendant in v8(map:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant.Name == "PalletPoint" and descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local model = descendant:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")

                        if model then
                            if not tbl23[model] then
                                tbl23[model] = {}
                            end

                            insert(tbl23[model], descendant)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)()

            local v87, v88, v89 = v6(tbl23)
            local v90 = v89
            local v91 = v87
            local v92 = v88

            while true do
                local v93, v94 = v91(v92, v90)
                if v93 == nil then
                    break
                end

                for k, v95 in v8(v94) do
                    v11(function()
                        palletDropEvent:FireServer(v95)
                    end)
                end

                v90 = v93
            end
        end,
    })

        Callback = function()
            if selectedPlayer and selectedPlayer ~= "" then
                local v102 = Players:FindFirstChild(selectedPlayer)

                if v102 and v102.Character and v102.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local character = localPlayer.Character

                    if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = v102.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + new2(0, 3, 0)
                    end
                else
                    v23("Player tidak ditemukan / tidak punya karakter")
                end

                return
            end

            v23("Pilih pemain terlebih dulu!")
        end,
    })

                    while SusR6Enabled and v105 and v105.Character do
                        local humanoidRootPart = v105.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        end
                        local n3 = humanoidRootPart.CFrame * new3(0, 0, -2.5)

                        TweenService:Create(humanoidRootPart2, new10(0.15), {CFrame = n3}):Play()
                        wait(0.15)
                        TweenService:Create(humanoidRootPart2, new10(0.15), {CFrame = n4}):Play()
                        wait(0.15)
                    end
                end)()

                susCoroutine = v107
            else

                    susAnimTrack:Stop()
                end

                susAnimTrack = nil
                susCoroutine = nil
            end
        end,
    })

    GetSuckedEnabled = false
    local connection7 = nil
    local v107 = nil
    local gravity = v2.Gravity

    tbl20.GetSucked = v104:AddToggle({
        Name = "Ew Player",
        Default = false,
        ConfigKey = "get_sucked",
        Callback = function(arg)
            GetSuckedEnabled = arg

            if arg then
                if not v105 then
                    tbl20.GetSucked.Set(false)
                    return
                end
                local character = localPlayer2.Character
                local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local character2 = v105.Character
                local humanoidRootPart2 = character2 and v105.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart2 then
                    gravity = v2.Gravity
                    v2.Gravity = 0

                    spawn(function()
                        while GetSuckedEnabled and humanoidRootPart.Position.Y <= 44 do
                            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame * new3(0, 1.5, 0)
                            wait()
                        end
                    end)

                    wait(1)

                    connection7 = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
                        if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart2 then
                            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart2.CFrame * new3(0, 0, -1.1) * angles(0, 3.141592653589793, 0)
                            humanoidRootPart.Velocity = new2(0, 0, 0)
                        end
                    end)

                    local Animation = new("Animation")
                    Animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://148840371"
                    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                    if humanoid then
                        v107 = humanoid:LoadAnimation(Animation)
                        v107:Play()
                        v107:AdjustSpeed(3)
                    end
                end
            else
                v2.Gravity = gravity

                if connection7 then
                    connection7:Disconnect()
                end

                if v107 then
                    v107:Stop()
                end

                connection7 = nil
                v107 = nil
            end
        end,
    })
